moḻi paṟṟiya camayac cārpaṟṟa kōṭpāṭukaḷi moḻi varalāṟṟaṟiñar aḻakāṉa cellappeyarkaḷiṭṭu vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷaṉar, ‘kūkūk’ koḷki (kṣaṭiṟa-ēṭiṟa vānaṭicala), `ākākā’k koḷki (luṭihṭihṭi vānaṭicala), ‘maṇi ōci’k koḷki (ṉuipē ṉuṭipē kūhnaṭicala), ‘tillāṉāk koḷki ’ allatu `āṭalpāṭaṟ koḷki’ (ḻanalanṭi-ninṭi vānaṭicala) `cikik koḷki’ (ḻunaḷaviuraṭayaviṭin vānaṭicala), `maḻalik koḷki’ (ūhṭiṉa ṭayapērayapana vānaṭicala), eṉa ivi peyariṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.*
vilaṅkolikaḷik kaṇṭu piṉpaṟṟiya etirolik kuṟiyīṭukaḷāka moḻi toṭaṅkiṟṟu eṉpatu ‘kūkūk’ koḷki. makkaḷ makiḻcci, viyappu uvaki ākiya uṇarccikaḷ kāraṇamāka iyaṟkiyāka eḻunta meyp pāṭṭolikaḷē moḻiyāyiṟ ṟeṉpatu ‘ākākā’k koḷki. maṇiyiṉ ceyaṟpāṭu ‘ṭāṇṭāṇ’ eṉṟa oliyāka eḻuvatupōla, ovvoru poruḷilum ceyalilum eḻum oliyiyē makkaḷ mutalil collākak koṇṭaṉar eṉpatu ‘maṇi ōci’k koḷki. itip puṉinturittavar camaṟkirutap perumpulavarum āriya iṉa moḻiyārāycciyāḷarumāṉa māk° mullar. makkaḷ makiḻccip pāṭalkaḷiliruntu moḻi piṟanta teṉpatu ‘tillāṉā’k koḷki.
kuḻantip pēcciliruntum cikiyuṭaṉ eḻum olikaḷi liruntumē moḻi tōṉṟiṟṟu eṉpatu ‘maḻalik koḷki’, ‘cikik koḷki’ ākum.
mēṟkūṟiya ellāvakiyilumē moḻiyil coṟkaḷ tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa eṉpatu uṇmi. moḻiyiṉ toṭakka nilikaḷik kuḻantikaḷiṉ maḻali cuṭṭikkāṭṭum paṇpuṭiyatu eṉpatum ottukkoḷḷak kūṭiyatē. āṉāl, ivaṟṟuḷ enta oru koḷkiyum taṉiyākavō, ellāk koḷkiyum kūṭṭākavōkūṭa, moḻiyiṉ piṟappi viḷakkap pōtiyaṉa alla; avi muṟkāla vaḷarccip paṭikaḷiyē cuṭṭikkāṭṭuvaṉa.
maṉita iṉap piṟappukku muntiyatu moḻiyiṉ piṟappu
moḻiyiṉ piṟappup paṟṟi moḻi nūlaṟiñarkaḷ kuḻappamaṭivataṟku oru muṉippāṉa kāraṇam uṇṭu. moḻiyiṉ piṟappu uṇmiyil maṉita iṉattiṉ piṟappukku muntiyatu. ataṉiārāyntu muṭivukāṇum nūl tuṟi moḻi nūlum aṉṟu. maṉita iṉa nūlum aṉṟu; uyir nūlē! ataṉ muṭivukāṇa vēṇṭiyavarum moḻi nūlaṟiñarallar, uyir nūlaṟiñarē!
moḻi iyaṟkiyōṭu oṉṟupaṭṭu maṉitaṉi iyakku vataṉ maṟi tiṟavu ituvē. tavira, maṉita iṉattukkut tāymoḻi uyiriṉum iṉṟiyamiyāc ciṟappuṭiyatā yiruppatum itaṉālēyē.
tāymoḻi evvaḷavu paṇpaṭāta moḻiyāy irun tālum ataṟkuriya iṉattukku ataṉiṉum ciṟanta uyirppaṇpu piṟitilli. evvaḷavu arumperum paṇpukaḷi uṭiyatāyiruntālum ayal moḻi uyirp paṇpō, ūkkum paṇpōkūṭa uṭiyatāka māṭṭātu. aḵtu iṉatti aḻikkum paṇpukaḷuḷ taliciṟantatākavē amiyum.
moḻiyiṉ nāṉku vaṭivaṅkaḷ
moḻiyiṉ vaḷarcciyil nām nāṉku vaṭivaṅkaḷik kāṇalām. avi uṇarcci vaṭivam, karuttu vaṭivam, oli vaṭivam, varivaṭivam, allatu eḻuttu vaṭivam eṉpaṉa. nāṉkum maṉita nākarikattil tirintu vaḷarntuvarukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl nāṉkil iṟutiyāṉa eḻuttuvaṭivam maṉita nākarikattil mikavum aṇimikkālattiya ceyti eṉṉalām.
maṉita iṉattiṉ nūṟāyirak kaṇakkāṉa āṇṭiya vāḻvil, tamiḻilkūṭa eḻuttu ēṟpaṭṭu eṇṇāyirattukkum aiyāyirattukkum iṭippaṭṭa āṇṭukaḷē ākiyuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu mēliyulaka ārāyccikaḷ terivikkiṉṟaṉa. mikappala iṉaṅkaḷ vakiyil, eḻuttu vaṭivam ēṟpaṭṭu nūṟṟāṇṭuk kaṇakkukaḷē ākiyuḷḷaṉa. cilavaṟṟukku iṉṉum eḻuttu amiyavilli.
tamakkeṉṟu taṉiyāka eḻuttamittukkoḷḷum muyaṟciyil īṭupaṭṭa iṉaṅkaḷ ulakil mika mikac cila-tamiḻiṉam, cīṉam, cumēr, ekiptu ākiyavi maṭṭumē! iṉṟiya ulakiṉ ellā moḻikaḷiṉ ellā eḻuttu muṟikaḷum ivviṉaṅkaḷ vakutta eḻuttu muṟikaḷiṉ aṭiccuvaṭṭip piṉpaṟṟiya virivukaḷē yākum. eḻuttu nīṅkalāṉa moḻiyiṉ mūṉṟu vaṭivaṅkaḷum maṉita nākarikattil mikavum tiruttamuṟṟu vaḷarntuḷḷaṉa vāyiṉum, tōṟṟattilum vaḷarcciyiṉ perum pakutiyilum, avi maṉitarkkum, uyiriṉaṅkaḷukkum potuvāṉavi ākum. avaṟṟiṉ piṟappu maṉita iṉattiṉ piṟappukkup pallūḻi, paṉṉūṟāyira āṇṭu muṟpaṭṭatu.
oli vakikaḷ: cikikaḷ 
oli vaṭivi oli, ōci, kural, pēccu eṉa nāl vakippaṭuttalām.
maṉitaṉ pēccu poruḷuṭiya, karuttuṭiya, karut tārāyvut tiṟaṉuṭiya oli. kiḷi, pūvi ākiya paṟavikaḷiṉ pēccum, kuyil mutaliya paṟavikaḷiṉ pāṭalum pōṉṟavi poruḷaṟṟa pēccuppōli; pāṭṭup pōli. ēṉiya vilaṅkolikaḷ, paṟavi olikaḷ ellām veṟuṅ kuralkaḷ. kākkiyiṉ karivu, kōḻiyiṉ kūval, ciṅkattiṉ muḻakkam, puliyiṉ uṟumal, kāḷiyiṉ ukkāram allatu ekkāḷam, yāṉiyiṉ piḷiṟal, kuraṅkiṉ calacalappu eṉa nām ivaṟṟi avaṟṟiṉ oliyuruviṉ vakiyiṉālēyē vēṟṟumip paṭuttu kiṟōm. eṉavē, ivvolikaḷ poruḷaṟṟaṉavi yāyiṉum uruvaṟṟavi alla eṉpatu teḷivu. kāṟṟoli, nīroli, aliyoli ākiyavaṟṟip pōṉṟatalla avikaḷ. avi uyirc ciṉṉaṅkaḷ; uyiriṉ muyaṟciyāl iṉa uṇarcci kāṭṭi eḻupavi.
ellā uyiriṉaṅkaḷukkumē kural uṇṭu eṉṟu palar eṇṇiviṭuva tuṇṭu. iḵtu uṇmi nili aṉṟu. mikac ciṟiya uyiriṉaṅkaḷil tavaḷi kattum, pāmpu cīṟum, palli pēcum, kocu iriyum! tēṉīkkaḷkūṭa muralum! ciṟṟuyirkaḷuḷ eṟumpukaḷ ētō oru vaki melloliyum ciki muṟikaḷum uṭiyavi eṉṟu teriyavarukiṟatu. āṉāl, eṟumpu uruviltāṉ ciṟitu; ciṟṟiṉaṅkaḷiṉ vaḷarcci muṟiyil aḵtu iḻinilippaṭṭataṉṟu. āyiṉum, ivvuyiriṉaṅkaḷukku aṭuttapaṭiyiluḷḷa kīḻiṉa uyirkaḷil, puḻuviṉaṅkaḷil, nīrp pāmpiṉaṅkaḷilkūṭa nām oliyiyum kēṭka muṭiyātu, kuraliyum kēṭka muṭiyātu.
poruḷ nōkki vaḷarum kuralit tamiḻ moḻi kuḻantikaḷiṉ kutalip pēccu, maḻalip pēccu eṉa vēṟu pirittu eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṟatu.
moḻi cikikaḷiliruntu piṟakkak kūṭumāṉālum, ataṉ uyir vaṭivam oliyē. mēlum uṇarcciyi veḷippaṭuttum meyppāṭukaḷuḷ tām ciki muṟpaṭṭatāyirukka muṭiyum. uṇarcciyi iṉa uyirkkut teriyappaṭuttum kuṟiyīṭu eṉṟa muṟiyilō, iṉa uyirkku atu teriya varum kuṟiyīṭu eṉṟa muṟiyilō ciki muṟpaṭṭatāyirukka muṭiyātu. ēṉeṉil, ciki uḷḷattil eṇṇi muṉ niṟpavarkku maṭṭum aṟivuṟuttat takkatu. oli eṇṇāmalē eḻuvatu; nāṟṟici yilum arukirukkum iṉap parappukkut tantiyaṭikkat takkatu.
moḻiyiṉ piṟappu oli vaṭivilēyē eṉṟum, ciki ataṉ toṭakka vaḷarccikku utaviya paṇpu maṭṭumē eṉṟum itaṉāl kāṇalām.
ūmarum ceviṭarum iṉṟu ciki moḻiyāṭukiṉṟaṉar. itupōlavē toṭakka uyirkaḷ oliyāl terivikka muṭiyāta karuttukaḷic ciki moḻiyāl vaḷarttirukkak kūṭum eṉṉalām. cikikaḷ oli vaḷarccikku muṟpaṭṭa iṭamum ituvē.
uṇarccip paṭiyiluḷḷa iyaṟki moḻinili kaṭantu, karuttu niliyiluḷḷa aṟivuppaṭikkuc cellum kaṭṭattil, cikiyē moḻiyi iṭṭucceṉṟu vaḷarkka utaviyirukka muṭiyum.
oru moḻiyāḷar iṉṉoru moḻiyip pēca muyalum iṭaṅkaḷil, iṉṟum oliyiṉ kuṟipāṭṭic cikiyum, cikiyiṉ nuṇṇuruvāṉa oliyaḻuttamum cērntu niṟivupaṭuttuvatu kāṇalām.
oli vaṭiviṉ piṟappu : kuṭumpam : camutāyam
uyiriṉaṅkaḷuḷ oliyiṉ piṟappukkum vaḷarppukkum utaviya paṇpukaḷ iraṇṭu-oṉṟu kuṭumpam, maṟṟoṉṟu camutāyam.
mutaṉ mutal oli, kural piṟappatu kuṭumpa vāḻvilētāṉ. āṇ, peṇ nēcat toṭarpilētāṉ! itu kuṟippiṭattakka, kavaṉikkappaṭavēṇṭiya, mukkiyattuvam uṭiya ceyti ākum. oliyilāt toṭakka uyiriṉaṅkaḷil āṇ, peṇpāliṉa vēṟupāṭē kiṭiyātu. avvēṟupāṭu ēṟpaṭṭa piṉṉarum kuṭumpa vāḻvu-ōr āṇ, ōr peṇ iṇivu-ariyatoru muṉṉēṟṟamākavē kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
	“kātaṉ maṭappiṭiyōṭum
	 kaḷiṟu varuvaṉa kaṇṭēṉ”
eṉṟu toṭaṅkit tirunāvukkaracar civaṉ-cakti vaṭivāka āṇ, peṇ cōṭi uyiriṉaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟik kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār. ivaṟṟuḷ yāṉiyum viṭiyum, māṉum, paṉṟiyum pōṉṟa vilaṅkukaḷum; kōḻi, kuyil, nāri, mayil, makaṉṟil, kiḷi mutaliya paṟavikaḷum; pūcciyiṉattil vaṇṭum iṭam peṟukiṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟu iṇiyiṇiyākak kuṟikka muṭiyāta uyiriṉaṅkaḷ pala uṇṭu. tavira eṟumpu, tēṉī mutaliya iṉaṅkaḷ iṇiyiṇiyāka vāḻvatilli. camutāyamē oru kuṭumpamāka vāḻkiṉṟaṉa. camutāyak kuṭumpattil cila camayam oru peṇṇukkup pala āṇkaḷ allatu ōr āṇukkup pala peṇkaḷ allatu ōr āṇukku ellāp peṇkaḷum eṉṟa oḻuṅkamiti muṟikaḷ payilkiṉṟaṉa.
kuṭumpa uyirkaḷilum, camutāya uyirkaḷilum kātaṟ paruvamē taṉik kuralukkuriya paruvamāy amikiṟatu. kuṭumpa vāḻvil ciṟanta palvakip paṟaviyiṉaṅkaḷilum ittakiya āṇ, peṇ aḻippu etir aḻippuk kuralkaḷit teḷivākak kēṭkalām. tavira, maḻik kālattil iraṭṭik kataṟalākac cevikaḷit tuḷikkum tavaḷiyiṉ iriccal, uṇmiyil āṇ tavaḷikaḷiṉ aṉpaḻippum, peṇ tavaḷikaḷiṉ aṉpēṟpumākiya kuralkaḷēyākum.
paṟavikaḷ : yāṉi : kuraṅku : maṉitaṉ
āṇ, peṇ toṭarpil vaḷarnta kural tāy, piḷḷi toṭarpilum tanti, piḷḷikaḷ toṭarpilum kiḷittu, iṉa vāḻvu allatu camutāya vāḻvil paṭarntatu. inniliyil olikaḷ perukiṉa. cikikaḷ oliyuṭaṉ iṇintu nāḷaṭivil oli māṟupāṭukaḷukkum, tiripukaḷukkum vaḻiceytaṉa. kuṭumpa vāḻviṉ uṇarcciyil piṟanta olikaḷ ciki pāvaṉikaḷ ākiyavi avaṟṟuṭaṉ toṭarpaṟṟa vāḻviṉ uṇarcci kaḷilum payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. itupōlavē, iṉavāḻvil toṭaṅkiya olikaḷum, cikikaḷum, pāvaṉikaḷum iṉaṅkaṭantu piṟa uyiriṉat toṭarpukaḷilkūṭap payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. nāyiṉ kaṇṇoḷiyilum, vāl kuḻippilum, muṇumuṇuppilum kuṭumpac cūḻal, iṉaccūḻal kaṭanta naṭpuṇarcciyiṉ ciki, pāvaṉi olikaḷik kāṇalām.
kuṭumpa vāḻvu maṭṭum uṭiya uyiriṉaṅkaḷum uṇṭu. camutāya vāḻvu maṭṭum uṭiya uyiriṉaṅkaḷum uṇṭu. iraṇṭum oruṅku vāynta uyiriṉaṅkaḷum uṇṭu. kuṭumpa vāḻvuc ciṟappumaṭṭum uṭiyaviyē paṟavikaḷuḷ perumpālāṉavi. avi mūḷi vaḷarcciyil ciṟakkavilli. āyiṉum, avaṟṟiṉ pāṭṭil potuvākavum, cilavaṟṟiṉ pēccilum maṉitaṉ īṭupaṭuvatuṇṭu. pēcum paṟavikaḷukku maṉitaṉukkuriya oliyuṟuppukaḷ vaḷarntum, cila camayam maṉitaṉiviṭa avi mēṟpaṭṭiruntum, mūḷi vaḷarcci yillāmiyiṉālēyē moḻi avaṟṟiṭiyē mutircci yaṭiyavilli.
kuṭumpa vāḻvum camutāya vāḻvum iṇiyum iṭat tilēyē mūḷi vaḷarcci mikuti. ittakiya iṇi vāḻvuyir kaḷuḷ mukkiyamāṉavi yāṉi, kuraṅku, maṉita iṉam ākiya mūṉṟumē. vilaṅkukaḷiṭiyē yāṉiyiṉ mūḷi vaḷarcciyum aṟivum kiṭṭattaṭṭa maṉitaṉukku aṭuttapaṭiyāyuḷḷatu. iraṇṭu mūṉṟu nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷukku mēṟpaṭṭa ataṉ nīṇṭa vāḻvum itaṟkup pērutaviyāyuḷḷatu.
maṉitaṉ mūḷi vaḷarccikkum aṟivukkum oruṅkē utaviya paṇpu cikikaḷukkuriya avaṉ uṭal vāyppē. itaṉik kuraṅkiṉattil mikuti kāṇkiṟōm. toḻil ceyvataṟkeṉṟē taṅku taṭiyaṟṟa vāyppuṭiya ivviraṇṭiṉaṅkaḷiṉ kikaḷum nimirnta uṭalum cikikaḷi ūkki mūḷi vaḷarccikku utaviṉa. mūḷiyiṉ karuttuppaṭi ceyalāṟṟi vāḻvi vaḷamākkavum ivi utaviṉa.
paṟaviyaḷavu uṇarcci nayamō, kural vāyppō, yāṉiyaḷavu mūḷittiṟamō kuraṅkiṉattukkuk kiṭiyātu. āṉāl, iraṇṭu iṉattukkum illāta aḷavil uṇarcci, kural nayam, mūḷi ākiya mūṉṟiyum vaḷarkka maṉita iṉattukku utaviya paṇpu kuraṅkiṉ cikip paṇpu allatu ceyaṟpaṇpēyākum.
moḻiyiṉ oṉṟupaṭuttum paṇpum vēṟupaṭuttum paṇpum
camutāya vaḷarcciyil oṟṟumip paṇpum kaṭṭuppāṭum evvaḷavu tēviyō, atē aḷavukku vēṟṟumip paṇpum atāvatu taṉi urimip paṇpum mikavum iṉṟiyamiyāt tēviyēyākum. taṉimaṉitariṉ taṅku taṭiyaṟṟa vaḷarcciyi ṉālēyē oṟṟumi payaṉuḷḷa oṟṟumiyāy, palvakip perukkamuḷḷa vaḷamāṉa oṟṟumiyāy amiyum. oṟṟumi allatu kaṭṭup pāṭu palvakip perukka vāyppu allatu taṉṉurimi ākiya inta iraṇṭu paṇpukaḷum cērntatē kūṭṭuḻippu. maṉita nākarikattiṉ aṭippaṭi ituvē.
moḻiyil nām inta iraṇṭu paṇpukaḷiyum vaḷamākak kāṇkiṟōm. iraṇṭu paṇpukaḷiyum moḻi iyalpāka vaḷarkkiṟatu.
orē moḻi pēcum makkaḷi moḻiyē ōr iṉamāka oṟṟumippaṭutti vaḷarkkiṟatu. ivvoṟṟumi taṉi maṉitar eṇṇikki kaṭanta potu oṟṟumi maṭṭumaṉṟu; atu talimuṟi kaṭanta talimuṟiyāṉa marapu oṟṟumiyum, moḻiyiṉ inta āṟṟal mikka aka oṟṟumiyē ākum. moḻikku veḷiyē maṟṟa moḻiyiṉaṅkaḷiliruntu ovvoru moḻiyiṉattiyum vēṟupaṭuttavum utavukiṟatu.
iv vēṟupāṭu kaṭantu nākarikamākiya paṭarkoṭi vaḷarum. āṉāl, paṇpil irumoḻikaḷ eḷitil iṇiyātu. eṉiṉum, mika nīṇṭa kālat toṭarpiṉāl moḻikaḷiṉ paṇpukaḷ caricamaniliyil oṉṟiliruntu oṉṟukku uṭalmāṟip pukuntu, nāḷaṭivil iru moḻiyiṉaṅkaḷum irumoḻikaḷil iṇinta ōr oṭṭuc ceṭiyāy, iru paṇpukaḷum iṇinta putup paṇpāka vaḷara iṭam uṇṭu. itu potumuṟi nikaḻvaṉṟu, arunikaḻvē. attuṭaṉ iṇiviṉ kāla nīṭṭippiṉ aḷavilēyē putuvāḻviṉ nīṭṭippum amiyamuṭiyum.
moḻi elli kaṭanta oṟṟumi vēṟṟumip paṇpip pōlavē, moḻiyiṉuḷḷum oṟṟumi vēṟṟumip paṇpukaḷik kāṇalām. moḻiyil kālattukkuk kālam uru vēṟupaṭuvatuṇṭu. iṭattukku iṭam uru vēṟupāṭu oru kālattilēyē nikaḻvatu muṇṭu. cilacamayam orē kālattil orē iṭattilumkūṭak kuḻuvukkuk kuḻu vēṟupāṭu ēṟpaṭakkūṭum.
im mūvaki māṟupāṭukaḷilum kālamāṟupāṭu oṉṟē iyaṟkiyāy eḻuvatu. maṟṟa iraṇṭum makkaḷ vāḻviṉ nerukka nekiḻvut toṭarpukaḷiṉ puṟac ciṉṉaṅkaḷākavē iyalkiṉṟaṉa.
moḻiyiṉ kāla māṟupāṭum vēṟupāṭum
kāla māṟupāṭṭi ilakkaṇam uṇarttātu. moḻi nūl maṭṭumē uṇarttum. aṭikkaṭi moḻi nūlār kāla māṟupāṭṭi aṭiyaṭutta peyarāl cuṭṭuvar. iraṇṭāyira āṇṭukku muṉṉirunta tamiḻ vaṭivip paḻantamiḻ eṉṟum, āyira āṇṭukku muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻ vaṭivi iṭikkālat tamiḻ eṉṟum iṉṟiya taṟkālat tamiḻiliruntu pirittuṇarvar. ivi orē moḻiyiṉ toṭarvaṭivāṉatāl tamiḻar vaḻakkil orē peyarkoṇṭaṉa. āṉāl, maliyāḷam mutaliya tamiḻiṉa moḻikaḷilum inti, āṅkilam mutaliya piṟa ulaka moḻikaḷilum paḻiya inti, paḻiya āṅkilam mutaliyaṉa iṉṟiya vaḻakku moḻiyiliruntu mikap pēraḷavil vēṟupaṭṭa ayaliṉa moḻikaḷ ākiyuḷḷaṉa.
paḻiya maliyāḷam eṉṟa toṭar uṇmiyil paḻantamiḻiyē kuṟikkum. paḻiya āṅkilamō āṅkilattiviṭa jermaṉ moḻikkē aṇittāṉatu.
iṉṟiya āṅkilattiliruntu paḻiya āṅkilam olippu muṟi, col tokuti, poruḷ, paṇpu ākiya ellā vaṟṟilumē vēṟupaṭṭa oru moḻiyākum. ainnūṟu āṇṭu kaḷukku muṟpaṭṭa cācar kāla moḻikūṭa, iṉṟiya āṅkila moḻiyiliruntu olippil evvaḷavu vēṟupaṭṭatu eṉpatik kīḻvarum cācariṉ mutalaṭikaḷāl kāṇalām. avvaṭikaḷiṉ cācar kāla, ikkāla olippu muṟikaḷ kīḻē tarappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
Whan that Aprille with his shoures soote 
The droughte of Merche hath perced to the roote.
poruḷ: ēpral mātattiṉ maḻi mārccu māta vaṟaṭciyi muṟṟilum ūṭuruviya kālattilē—.
taṟkāla olippumuṟi
veṉ teṭ ēpril vit hi° ṣavar° °vīṭ
te ṭruṭ av mārc hat piyēr°ṭ ṭu ti rūṭ
cācar kāla olippu muṟi
hvaṉ taṭ aprillē
	vit hi° ṣūra° sōṭṭē,
te ṭrūḵṭ av merc
	hat persaṭ ṭō te rōṭṭē!
irukāla vaḻakkiṉiṭiyē yāppu muṟiyum cantamumē muṟṟilum māṟupaṭṭuviṭṭaṉa eṉṟu kāṇalām. cācariṉ aḻakiya iṉṉōcip pāṭal iṉṟiya olippu muṟiyil antac cantaṅ keṭṭa ōr urinaṭit tuṇukkāka māṟiviṭṭiruk kiṉṟatu! cācariṉ cantam iṉṟiya āṅkila moḻiyiṉ cantati taviṭa nam tamiḻc cantattukkē aṇimiyuṭiyatu eṉṉalām!
kāla māṟupāṭṭāl moḻiyiṉ vaṭivam maṭṭumaṉṟip peyarum māṟupaṭṭuviṭṭāl, orumoḻi iṉṉoru moḻiyākiviṭṭatāka nām koḷkiṟōm.
maliyāḷa nāṭṭup paḻantamiḻ iṉṟu maliyāḷamākavum atu pōlavē kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭu, āntira nāṭṭup paṇṭit tamiḻ vaḻakkukaḷ iṉṟu kaṉṉaṭa teluṅku moḻikaḷ ākavum māṟiyatu ivvāṟēyākum.
tici vaḻakkukaḷ
moḻi vaḻaṅkum ellikkuḷ iṭattukkiṭam vēṟupāṭu ēṟpaṭuvatu iyalpu. vāḻkkit toṭarpu nerukkamāy uḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷil ivvēṟupāṭu kuṟivu. itu taḷarvuṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷilum, toṭarpu kuṉṟiya iṭaṅkaḷilum, vēṟupāṭu paṭippaṭiyāka vaḷarkiṟatu. yāḻppāṇat tamiḻ, tirunelvēlit tamiḻ, tañcit tamiḻ, kōyamuttūr tamiḻ ākiya vēṟupāṭukaḷum, teṉ tiruvāṅkūr maliyāḷam, vaṭa tiruvāṅkūr-kocci maliyāḷam, malapār maliyāḷam ākiya vēṟupāṭukaḷum ittakiyaviyē.
pala kūṟukaḷil moḻikaṭantu tici vaḻakkukaḷ iṉa moḻikaḷi iṇippatukūṭa aritaṉṟu. yāḻppāṇat tamiḻum teṉ tiruvāṅkūr maliyāḷamum orupuṟam, kōyamuttūrt tamiḻum malapār maliyāḷamum maṟṟoru puṟam cilapala kūṟukaḷil ottiyalvatu kāṇalām. nila iyalkūṟu itaṉ kāraṇaṅkaḷit teḷivākak kāṭṭukiṟatu. teṟkē āralvāymoḻi, āriyaṅkāvuk kaṇavāykaḷiṉ mūlam teṉtamiḻkkum teṉ maliyāḷattuk kum toṭarpu ēṟpaṭa vaḻi uṇṭu. atupōla vaṭakkē pālakkāṭṭuk kaṇavāy mūlam vaṭatamiḻkkum vaṭa maliyāḷattukkum toṭarpu ēṟpaṭa vaḻiyuṇṭu. maṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷil iṭi niṉṟu pirikkum mēṟku malit toṭarē, moḻiyiṉ vāḻvu taḷarnta kālaṅkaḷil tamiḻ-maliyāḷa moḻi vēṟupāṭṭukkup peritum kāraṇamāyiṟṟu eṉpatiyum itu teḷivupaṭuttum.
tolkāppiyar tamkālap paṇṭit tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ iṭavēṟu pāṭukaḷit ‘ticimoḻi’, ‘ticic col’ eṉṟu kuṟittaṉar. camaṟkiruta ilakkaṇa uriyāciriyarkaḷum ipporuḷuṇarntu ivvaḻakkit ‘tēcyam’ eṉṟaṉar. āṉāl, iṭikkālattār im marapuṇarāmal taṭumāṟit ‘ticimoḻi’ eṉṟāl vēṟumoḻi atāvatu ayalmoḻi eṉṟu mayaṅkiṉar.
kuḻu moḻi, ceyaṟki moḻi
toḻil, celvanili, paṇpāṭu, marapu kāraṇamākat taṉittotuṅki vāḻpavarum kalantu vāḻpavarum uṇṭu. kīḻiyulakil piṟappiṉamum cātiyum ittakiyavi. itaṉāl, potuvāka eṅkum taṉiccāti vaḻakkukaḷum, nāṭṭuppuṟa vaḻakku nakara vaḻakku eṉṟa vēṟupāṭukaḷum tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa. tavira pulavar vaḻakku, māṇavar vaḻakku, toḻilāḷar vaḻakku, kuḻantikaḷ vaḻakku eṉap paruva, toḻil, paṇpu māṟu pāṭṭukkēṟpat taṉivaḻakkukaḷ uṇṭu.
nūlkaḷ eḻutuvōr iṉṟum pala tuṟic coṟkaḷ, tuṟi vaḻakkukaḷi uṭiyavarāvar.
ilakkiyattukkē peritum uriya coṟkaḷit tolkāppiyar uric coṟkaḷ eṉa vakuttār. piṟkālattār iṅkum avaṟṟip paṇpuc coṟkaḷ eṉṟu koṇṭu mayaṅkiṉar. ilakkiyattilum paḻimippaṭṭu aruvaḻakkāṉa vaṟṟit tolkāppiyar tiricol eṉṟār.
tavira, aṟivu nūlkaḷukkup potuvākavum camaya aṟivu nūlkaḷukkuc ciṟappākavum paṇṭit tamiḻaṟiñar vaḻaṅkiya coṟkaḷiyum, vaḻakkukaḷiyum tolkāppiyar kālat tamiḻar ‘vaṭa moḻi’,‘vaṭacol’ eṉa vakuttaṉar. piṟkālattār itaṉiyum camaṟkiruta moḻiyik kuṟippateṉṟu mayaṅki iṭarppaṭṭaṉar. tāmum iṭaṟit tamiḻariyum vaḻivaḻi iṭaṟuvittaṉar.


ulaka moḻiyiṉaṅkaḷ 
terinta poruḷiliruntē teriyāta poruḷi nōkki ārāycci ceytal vēṇṭum. iḵtu ārāycci muṟiyiṉ aṭippaṭi ilakkaṇam. itaṉi naṉku uṇarntavarkaḷ mēli ulaka muṟiyil ārāycciyi mēṟ koṇṭavarkaḷ. moḻiyiṉ piṟappil avarkaḷ uṭaṉaṭiyākak karuttuc celuttātataṟkuk kāraṇamum, paḻimiyiliruntu moḻi varalāṟu toṭaṅ kāmikku viḷakkamum ituvē.
moḻiyārāyccikkut toṭakkanili ātāraṅkaḷāy utavattakka mūlaṅkaḷ iraṇṭu vakippaṭṭavi. oṉṟu iṉṟirukkum moḻikaḷ, moḻi vaḻakkukaḷ, avaṟṟil kāṇappaṭum oṟṟumi vēṟṟumikaḷ ākiyavi. iṉṉoṉṟu moḻikaḷiṉ paḻimic ciṉṉaṅkaḷāṉa ilakkiyaṅkaḷum, paḻaṅkāla moḻikaḷiṉ vēṟu vakiyāṉa (iṭappeyar, āṭpeyar, kalveṭṭu kaḷ) mutaliya ciṉṉaṅkaḷum ākum. ivaṟṟiṉaṭippaṭiyāka ulaka moḻikaḷi aṟiñar moḻik kuḻukkaḷākavum, moḻiyiṉaṅkaḷākavum vakuttuḷḷaṉar.
ulakiṉ mukkiyamāṉa moḻiyiṉaṅkaḷ varumāṟu
1. 	piriṭṭaṉ mutal vaṅkāḷam variyuḷḷa nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa moḻikaḷ intiya-āriya moḻikaḷi uḷḷaṭakkiya intu-jermāṉiya moḻi iṉam ākum. āṅkilam, jermaṉ, pirañcu mutaliya taṟkāla airōppiya moḻikaḷum, ilattīṉ, kirēkkam mutaliya paṇṭi airōppiya moḻikaḷum paṇṭip pāracīkamum camaṟkirutamum iṉṟiya vaṭa intiyat tāymoḻikaḷum itaṉuṭpaṭṭavi.
2. 	iṉṟu āriya iṉa moḻikaḷ nākarika ulakil evvaḷavu parantuḷḷaṉavō avvaḷavu varalāṟṟukku muṟpaṭṭa (ki.mu. 3000 mutal ki.mu. 500 vari) ulakil paraviyirunta iṉam cemittiya iṉam ākum. iṉṟiya ekiptiya, arāpiya, yūta iṉaṅkaḷ itaṉ vaḻi vantavi. ivviṉa moḻikaḷ āriya moḻikaḷiviṭap paḻimi vāyntavi. airōppiya āriya moḻikaḷiṉ eḻuttu muṟikaḷum ilakkiyaṅkaḷum ivaṟṟiṉiṭa miruntu piṟantaviyē. airōppiyar paṇpāṭum kaliyiyalkaḷum camaya vāḻvumkūṭap peritum ivviṉaṅkaḷiṉ vaḻivantaviyē.
	māṇṭoḻinta naṭunilak kaṭalaka nākarikaṅkaḷil pala ivviṉa marapu cārntaviyē.
3. 	tamiḻ, maliyāḷam, kaṉṉaṭam, tuḷu, teluṅku mutaliya teṉṉāṭṭu moḻikaḷum pirākuvi, kōṇṭu, kūyi, irājmakāl mutaliya intiya mānilap paṇpaṭā moḻikaḷum tirāviṭa iṉam cārntavi. cemittiya iṉattuṭaṉum paṇṭik kāla naṭunilak kaṭalaka iṉattuṭaṉum ivi neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭiyavi eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
4. 	airōppāvil piṉlāntu mutal kiḻakku uruciyā variyil parantu citaṟik kiṭakkum moḻikaḷ intiya moḻikaḷ eṉṟum iraṣyāvukkum turukkikkum iṭiyē parantu citaṟik kiṭakkum moḻikaḷ turāṉiya moḻikaḷ eṉṟum kuṟikkappaṭuvatuṇṭu. ivi aṉittum ōriṉamākalām allatu ōriṉat toṭar puṭiyaṉavākalām. aṟiñar kālṭuvel tirāviṭa moḻi iṉam intac cittiya, turāṉiya iṉa moḻikaḷuṭaṉ toṭarpuṭiyavi eṉṟu karutiṉār. immoḻikaḷum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷum potup paṇpuṭiyaviyāyiṉum, avaṟṟi iṉat toṭar puṭiyavi eṉṟu koḷvataṟkilli eṉpatē iṉṟiya aṟivu ulakiṉ karuttu ākum.
5.	cīṉamoḻi maṅkōliya iṉamākak karutappaṭukiṟatu. teṉkiḻakkāci yāviluḷḷa kocciṉ cīṉā, kampōṭiyā, cayām, parmā ākiyavaṟṟiṉ moḻikaḷ ittuṭaṉ toṭarpuṭiyavi. ivi aṉṉamiya iṉameṉṟum kuṟippiṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
6. 	vaṅkam mutal, antamāṉ mutaliya tīvukaḷiṉ vaḻiyākat teṉkiḻakkāciyāvil paraviya ‘māṉkimō’ moḻi iṉam tirāviṭa moḻikaḷuṭaṉ muṟkālaṅkaḷil kalanta ōriṉamākak kuṟikkappaṭukiṟatu. ivi ā°tirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭiyiṉattuṭaṉ toṭarpuṭiyavi eṉak karutap paṭukiṉṟaṉa.
7. 	āppirikkap paḻaṅkuṭi moḻikaḷ.
8. 	amerikkap paḻaṅkuṭi moḻikaḷ.
9. 	ā°tirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi moḻikaḷ.
10.	vaṭāppirikka hāmittiya iṉam.
11. 	jappāṉ moḻi.
moḻi mūlam
maṉita iṉattil moḻiyiṉ piṟappup paṟṟik kaṟpaṉi ceytavarum kaṟpaṉiyuṭaṉ ārāycciyik kalakka viṭṭavarum palar. uṇmi etuvāyiṉum itu vaki muyaṟci maṉita uḷḷattil ārvam tūṇṭic cuviyūṭṭuvatākum eṉpatil aiyamilli.
maṉita iṉattiṉ moḻi mutalil oru moḻiyākat tōṉṟi, iṭik kālattilēyē palavākat tirinta teṉṟa kaṟpaṉi viviliya nūlil ōr aḻakiya kati uruvil kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. 
toṭakkak kāla maṉita iṉam vāṉulakukku oru kuṟukku vaḻi kāṇa muṟpaṭṭatu. in nōkkattuṭaṉ ellā maṉitarum oruṅku cērntu tiṭṭamiṭṭu, pāpilōṉil oru pāriya kōpuram eḻuppiṉar. kōpuram paṭippaṭiyāka uyarntu vāṉulakiyē aṇukat toṭaṅkiṟṟām! atu kaṇṭu cīṟṟam koṇṭa kaṭavuḷ iṭippaṭiyi aṉuppi atic citaṟaṭittārām!
mīṇṭum maṉita iṉam oṉṟupaṭṭuk kaṭavuḷ viruppatti etirkkātapaṭi, kaṭavuḷ maṉita iṉattiṉmītu paḻiyuri vīciṉārām! ‘maṉitar oruvar pēcci oruvar aṟiyātu pōkak kaṭavar! avarkaḷ moḻi palavākac citaṟak kaṭavatu!’ eṉṟu! avar vitittārām!
mēli nāṭukaḷilkūṭa ulakiṉ mūlamoḻi epirēya moḻi eṉṟu nampiyavar, annampikki ārvattuṭaṉ ārāycciyiliṟaṅki ataṟku ātaravu tēṭiyavar palar.
18ām nūṟṟāṇṭiliruntu āriya iṉa eḻucci mēli nāṭukaḷilum parantatu. ataṉ payaṉāka maṟṟōr ārva nampikki eḻuntatu. ulaka moḻikaḷiṉ mūla mutaṉ moḻi camaṟkirutam eṉpati nāṭṭap pala mēli ulaka aṟiñarkaḷ talippaṭṭaṉar. kīḻiyulakil āriya iṉap paṟṟāḷar ārvatti itu putuppittatu.
in nampikkikaḷum ārva ārāyccikaḷum iṉṟu pōli ārāyccikaḷākak kāṇappaṭṭuviṭṭaṉa.
kaṉṉit tāymoḻi
tamiḻē ulakiṉ mūla mutaṉ moḻi eṉṟu tamiḻk kaliñar, tamiḻaṟiñar toṉṟutoṭṭu iṉṟuvari karuti vantuḷḷaṉar. vātiṭṭum ataṟku ārāycciyaṭippaṭiyāṉa ātāraṅkaḷ tēṭiyum vantuḷḷaṉar. ittakiya ārāyccikaḷ perukat toṭaṅkiya kālattilvāḻntavarkaviñar pāratiyār.
“toṉṟu nikaḻntatu aṉittum uṇarntiṭu
 cūḻkali vāṇarkaḷum-ivaḷ
 eṉṟu piṟantavaḷ eṉṟu ṇarāta
 iyalpiṉa ḷām eṅkaḷ tāy!”
eṉṟu avar kaṭavuḷiyum tamiḻakattiyum tamiḻiyum oru niliyil vittup pāṭukiṟār. maṟṟum piṟavā moḻi, iṟavā moḻi, mūvāk kaṉṉit tāymoḻi eṉṟu tamiḻip pōṟṟāta kaviñarilli. tamiḻ paṟṟiya ārāyccikaḷ eḻumuṉṉarē avaṟṟik kaṉavu kaṇṭa kaviñar oruvar uṇṭu. avarē kavip pērāciriyar maṉōṉmaṇiyam cuntaraṉār.
	palluyirum pala ulakum
	paṭit taḷittut tuṭikkiṉum ōr
	elliyaṟu paramporuḷ muṉ
	iruntapaṭi iruppatupōl,
	kaṉṉaṭamum kaḷi teluṅkum
	kaviṉ maliyāḷamum tuḷuvum 
	uṉṉu tarat tutit teḻuntē
	oṉṟu pala āyiṭiṉum,
	āriyam pōl ulaka vaḻakku
	aḻin toḻintu citiyā uṉ
	cīriḷamit tiṟam viyantu
	ceyal maṟantu vāḻttutumē.
eṉṟu avar iṟavā moḻiyil tamiḻiṉ māṟā iḷami nalaṉip pōṟṟi yuḷḷār. ulaka ārāyccip pōkkukaḷum paḻimi yārāyccikaḷum cuntaraṉār, kaviñar pārati kāla mutalē ivvārvak kōṭpāṭṭukku ātaravu tarupaviyāka uḷḷaṉa. āyiṉum, moḻiyārāycci muṟippaṭi ikkaruttu muṭivāṉa karuttāka valiyuṟavu peṟṟuviṭṭatākak kūṟamuṭiyātu. ēṉeṉil, nām mēlē kuṟippiṭṭapaṭi ārāyccikaḷ terinta aṟiviliruntē teriyāta aṟivukku, muṭinta muṭiviliruntē muṭivupeṟā meymmikku, kaṇkaṇṭa iṉṟiya vāḻviliruntē iṟantakāla nilikkuc cellutal vēṇṭum. maṉita iṉat toṭakkam, moḻit toṭakkamvari ittakiya ārāycci iṉṉum nammi iṭṭuc cellavilli.
ārāycciyulakiṉ iṉṟiya niliyilum nām im muṭivu nōkkiya kīḻvarum kūṟukaḷi aiyantiripiṉṟi ēṟṟukkoḷḷa muṭiyum.
1.	tamiḻum tamiḻ moḻiyum mikamikap paḻimi vāyntavi. iṉṟu ulakil uyiruṭaṉ iyaṅkum moḻikaḷuḷ ataṉ paḻimiyiyō, paḻam perumiyiyō emmoḻiyum aṇukiyuḷḷatākak kūṟamuṭiyātu. paḻimiyil cīṉamoḻiyum, paḻamperumiyil kirēkka moḻiyum maṭṭumē ataṉuṭaṉ tolivāṉa niliyil oppiṭak kūṭiyavi.
2.	tamiḻ camaṟkirutattukku ūḻi pērūḻik kaṇakkil muṟpaṭṭa moḻi, attuṭaṉ aḵtu āriya iṉa ūḻikku muṟpaṭṭē uyar nākarika vāḻvu peṟṟiruntatu.
3.	tamiḻ āriya iṉa ūḻikku muṟpaṭṭa cemittiya naṭunilak kaṭalaka iṉaṅkaḷuṭaṉ camakālat toṭarpuṭiyatu. avaṟṟit tāṇṭiya paḻimiyum paḻam perumiyum uṭiyatu.
muṭivuṟāta inta ārāyccip pōkki nām iṉṉoru kōṇattiliruntum kāṇalām.
moḻikku muṟpaṭṭanili
maṉita ulakil nāṭṭukku nāṭu ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷa vēṟupāṭum nāṭukaḷiṉ ellik kōṭukaḷum toṉṟu toṭṭu uḷḷaṉa alla. nāṭāka uruvākāta, nāṭṭiṉamāka urup peṟāta nilap parappukaḷiyum makkaṭ parappukaḷiyum iṉṟukūṭa nām ulakil kāṇalām. nāṭupaṟṟiya inta meymmi, moḻi vakiyilum poruntuvatēyākum. ēṉeṉṟāl, mikap perumpālum moḻi yaṭippaṭiyākavē nāṭum, nāṭṭiṉamum uruvākiṉṟaṉa.
iṉṟu inti allatu hinti eṉṟu vaḻaṅkum moḻi appeyar peṟṟatu, inti allatu hinti, eṉṟa nilapparappiṉ aṭippaṭiyākavum ‘cintu’ eṉṟa āṟṟuppeyariṉ aṭip paṭiyākavumē. āyiṉum, inti pēcappaṭum parappu cintu āṟṟup parappaṉṟu, kaṅkiyāṟṟiṉ parappē. immuraṇ pāṭṭukkuk kāraṇam eṉṉa? 
cintu, kaṅki veḷimuḻutum aṇimivari nāṭāka uruvākā nilapparappākavum. moḻiyuruvam peṟāta pēccup parappākavumē iruntatu. cintu āṟṟukku mēṟkēyuḷḷa nākarika makkaḷ iru nilapparappiyum cintu allatu intu eṉṟum, ataṉ uruvākā moḻip parappi ‘inti’ eṉṟum vaḻaṅkiṉar. avvuruvākāp parappiṉ uyirmayyam allatu karumutalā yiruntu, mutaṉ mutal uruvāṉa moḻi kaṅki nilamoḻiyē. aḵtu inti eṉṟu peyar peṟṟa vaki ituvē.
‘inti’ moḻiyiṉ peyarukkuk kāraṇamāṉa cintu āṟṟup pakuti iṉṉum peritum uruvākāta moḻip parappākavē irukkiṟatu.
mēli airōppāvum, teṉṉāṭum, cīṉamum nīṅkalāṉa ulakiṉ mikap perumpakutiyiṉ iṉṟiya nili cintu āṟṟuppakutiyiṉ niliyē.
moḻikku muṟpaṭṭa ulakiṉ pēccup parappu niliyi ipparappukaḷ cuṭṭik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṉṟiya ulaka moḻikaḷiṉ uruvākā mūlap potu marapākavē maṉita ulakiṉ pēccu nili toṭakkattil iruntiruntatu eṉṉalām.
mūvā mutalarumpu
uruvākā moḻi mūlattiliruntu uruvāki mukiḻtta mutal, mutal moḻiyarumpu tamiḻ. ataṉi uyir mayyamākak koṇṭē piṟa arumpukaḷ nērkiḷi arumpukaḷākavum pakkakkiḷi arumpu kaḷākavum palkip perukiṉa. ellāk kiḷikaḷiṉ vāḻvukkum pakkak kiḷikaḷiṉ vāḻvukkum atu mayyamāka niṉṟu, avaṟṟiṉ vāḻvu tāḻvukaḷil toṭarpu koṇṭirunta taṉāltāṉ, aḵtu avi ellāvaṟṟiṉ uyir aṉupavaṅkaḷiyum paṇpākak koṇṭu, avi ellāvaṟṟiṉ vāḻviyum kaṭantu vāḻum takutipeṟṟirukka vēṇṭum eṉṉalām.
maṉita iṉa moḻikaḷiṉ pala talimuṟikaḷ vantu vantu māṟiyum, vāṭāt taṉiyarumpākat tamiḻ eṉṟum niṉṟu nilavuvataṉ maṟitiṟavu ituvē ākalām.
moḻi kaṭanta inta moḻiyiṉ vāḻvil ulaka moḻi nūlār iṉṉum carivarak kavaṉam celuttavilli. ataṉ taṉip paṇpukaḷiyum uṇarntu koḷḷavilli. ivviraṇṭilum īṭupaṭupavar maṉita iṉam kaṭanta oru maṉita ulakap paṇpukku vaḻivakuttavarāvar eṉpatu uṟuti.
tamiḻ moḻi, tamiḻilakkaṇa ilakkiyam, tamiḻp paṇpu ākiyavaṟṟi ṉūṭāka appaṇpukaḷit tēṭi ārāyvatu tamiḻ vaḷarppavarkku maṭṭumaṉṟi, ulakap paṇpu vaḷarppavarkkum taliyāya kaṭṭuppāṭu ākum.
aṭikkuṟippu :
	* 	ivaṟṟiṉ virivi ṭākṭar mu.varatarācaṉavarkaḷiṉ moḻi varalāṟu iyal 21. moḻiyiṉ tōṟṟam eṉṟa pakutiyil kāṇka. aṟiñar tēvanēyap pāvāṇariṉ mutal tāy moḻi muṉṉuri pakkam 24-il ivaṟṟi oppoli, uṇarvoli, kuṟiyoli eṉṟum vāyc ceykiyoli, kuḻanti vaḷarppoli eṉṟum pirittut tamiḻc coṟkaḷil mikap perum pālāṉavaṟṟiyum avaṟṟiṉaṭippaṭiyil virivāka vakukkiṟār. avar vakuppumuṟi ārāytaṟkuriyatu.


4. moḻiyiṉ vaḷarcci
ulakap poruḷkaḷi uyiruṭiyaṉa, uyirillātaṉa eṉṟu pakuttal marapu vaḻakku. āyiṉum, poruḷ eṉṟa vakiyil ellām orēvakit taṉip poruḷkaḷiṉ kalavi, cērkkikaḷālāṉaviyē. tiṭṭamaminta kalavi, cērkki, avaṟṟiṉ mūlamāṉa vaḷarcci taḷarcci, ivi kaṭanta iṉap perukkam, iṉa vaḷarcci ākiyaviyē uyirppaṇpiṉ vēṟupāṭṭuk kūṟukaḷ eṉṉalām.
uyirilāp poruḷkaḷiliruntu uyirpporuḷkaḷ tiṭumeṉat tōṟṟuvatilli. avaṟṟiṉ tōṟṟa vaḷarccikku utavum iyaṟkiyiṉ puṟa uyirp paṇpukaḷum uṇṭu. iyaṟki vaḻi niṉṟu uyiriṉaṅkaḷum maṉitaṉum peṟum aka uyirp paṇpukaḷum uṇṭu. katiravaṉoḷi, kāṟṟu, tī, maḻi, poruḷkaḷiṉ kalaviyāṟṟalkaḷ, cērkkiyāṟṟalkaḷ ākiyavi puṟa uyir āṟṟalkaḷ. ivaṟṟiyē paṇṭit tamiḻar aimperum pūtaṅkaḷ eṉa vakuttaṉar.
iṭikkāla camaṟkirutap paḻimi vāṇarum muṟkāla mēṉāṭṭup paḻimi vāṇarum ivaṟṟit taṉip poruḷkaḷ eṉṟu koṇṭu mayaṅkiṉar-iṉṟaḷavum mayaṅki varukiṉṟaṉar.
uyirkaḷiṉ taṉṉuṇarvu, avā, uṇarcci, aṟivu, ārāycci ākiyavi aka uyir āṟṟalkaḷ.
aka uyir āṟṟalkaḷiṉ aṭippaṭiyākavē kuṭumpam, camutāyam, iṉam eḻuntuḷḷaṉa. ivi putiya iṉa āṟṟalkaḷip paṭikkiṉṟaṉa. moḻi, camayam, kalikaḷ, iyalkaḷ, meyviḷakka ārāycci (ñāṭiṭiḷaṭiyīhla) ākiyavi ittakiya iṉa āṟṟalkaḷ. ivaṟṟuḷ moḻi aṭippaṭi iṉa āṟṟalākavum, piṟa iṉa āṟṟalkaḷit tōṟṟuvittu vaḷarkkum uyirppāṟṟalākavum amikiṉṟatu.


moḻi ōr uyiriyakkam
moḻi ōr uyiriyakkam eṉpar moḻi nūlār. iḵtu ōr aḻakiya collaṇi uvaki maṭṭumē eṉṟu koḷpavar uṇṭu. āṉāl, aḵtu uvami yuruvil uḷḷa uvaki kaṭanta uṇmi. āyvu nōkkuṭiyavarkkuc collaṇi uruvil aminta collaṇi kaṭanta oru meymmi. aḵtu āḻntaporuḷ potinta ōr aṟivārnta vaḻakku. moḻi meymmiyilēyē muṟṟilum uyirppaṇpuvāynta ōr uyir iyakkam ākum.
maṉitaṉ tiṭṭappaṭuttiṉālum, tiṭṭappaṭuttā viṭṭālum, aṟintālum aṟiyāviṭṭālum, moḻi avaṉ tiṭṭamum aṟivum uṭpaṭutti, palacamayam avaṉ tiṭṭamum aṟivum kaṭantu tāṉum vaḷarvatu, maṉita iṉattiyum vaḷarppatu.
moḻiyiyum, moḻi cārntu vaḷarum nāṭṭiyum iṉattiyum nām ‘tāy’ eṉṟu kuṟippatu mikiyaṇi vaḻakkaṉṟu. kuṟiyaṇi vaḻakkē eṉpati itu kāṭṭum. uṇmiyil tāyiṉ paṇikūṭa moḻi, nāṭu, iṉam ākiyavaṟṟiṉ paṇikaḷic cuṭṭikkāṭṭumēyaṉṟi avaṟṟukku īṭākamāṭṭātu.
tāy piḷḷiyip peṟukiṟāḷ. vaḷarttuviṭukiṟāḷ, avvaḷavē, piḷḷiyiṉ vāḻvuṭaṉ tāy vāḻvatilli. piḷḷiyiṉ piḷḷikaḷi, piḷḷiyiṉ piḷḷikaḷ talimuṟikaḷi vaḷarppatilli. moḻiyum, nāṭum iṉamum ivaṟṟit tiṟampaṭa āṟṟukiṉṟaṉa!
uyirkaḷaṟiyāmalē vaḷarum uyirpōla, moḻiyum tiṭṭappaṭuttuvārillāmalē tiṭṭamamintu tiṭṭampaṭa vaḷarvatu. atu vaḷarcci taḷarccikaḷ uṟuvatu maṭṭumaṉṟu. vaḷarcci taḷarccikaḷ tāṇṭi iṉam perukkuvatu, iṉap perukkam kaṭantu iṉam vaḷarppatu. 
uyiriṉaṅkaḷi atu maṉita iṉam nōkki vaḷappaṭutti, maṉita iṉatti kaṉavulakak kuṟikkōḷāṉa mī-maṉita iṉam nōkki uruvākki varukiṟatu.
pāriya kuṟiyīṭṭu iyantira amippu
uṇarcci, aṟivu, oli eḻuttu ākiya moḻiyiṉ nāṉku paṭiyāṉa vaṭivaṅkaḷil mikappintiya kaṭici vaṭivam eḻuttē. ellāp paṭikaḷilum nām ataṉ tōṟṟa vaḷarccikaḷi muḻutum kaṇittuk kāṇa muṭiyātu. āyiṉum, eḻuttu vaṭiviṉ vaḷarccikaḷē maṟṟavaṟṟiyum cuṭṭik kāṭṭuvaṉa.
pāriya iyantiraṅkaḷi iyakkum ciṉṉañ ciṟu kumiḻkaḷi nām pārttirukkiṟōm. iṅkē kumiḻkaḷ tammaḷavil āṟṟaluṭiyavi alla. āṟṟaluṭiya iṇippukaḷukku avi oru kuṟiyīṭu maṭṭumē, olipēci iṇippu niliyaṅkaḷil pala maṉita illaṅkaḷ, kiḷi niliyaṅkaḷ eṇkaḷāl kuṟikkappaṭṭu, ellā eṇkaḷum orē mukappup palakiyil koṇṭu patikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. anta oru mukappup palakiyi iyakki oru nakaram muḻuvatiyum allatu ōr ulakap pakuti muḻuvatiyum iyakka muṭikiṟatu.
moḻi ittakiya oru pāriya olipēci iṇippu niliyamē. maṉita mūḷi ataṉ kuṟiyīṭṭeṇkaḷi iṇikkum oru mukappup palaki pōṉṟatē yākum. ataṉ ovvoru collum oru kuṟiyīṭṭeṇ. ovvoru col tokutiyum oru kiḷi niliyam. ataṉ ilakkaṇam an niliyattiṉ piṉṉaliṇippukaḷiṉ mātirip paṭamēyaṉṟi vēṟaṉṟu. atu moḻikkut tiṭṭam amippatilli. moḻiyiṉ tiṭṭatti ārāyntu maṉitaṉukku aṟivuṟuttukiṟatu.
maṉitaṉ kaṇṭupiṭittuḷḷa, iyaṟṟiyuḷḷa, vakuttuḷḷa entap pāriya iyantirattiṉ kuṟiyīṭṭu muṟiyum moḻiyiṉ kuṟiyīṭṭumuṟikku īṭākātu. ēṉeṉṟāl, atu mikamikap pāriya, mikamikac cikkalkaḷ niṟinta oṉṟu. uṇmiyil atu kuṟiyīṭukaḷ, kuṟiyīṭṭiṉ maṟu kuṟiyīṭukaḷ, avaṟṟiṉ maṟupaṭik kuṟiyīṭukaḷ eṉap paṉmaṭiyāṉa, pallaṭukkāṉa kuṟiyīṭṭu muṟikaḷi uṭiyatu.
poruḷkaḷiṉ kuṟiyīṭākap pulaṉ uṇarvukaḷ amikiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ kuṟiyīṭukaḷāṉa uḷḷattiṉ uṇarvukaḷē karuttukaḷ. karuttiṉ kuṟiyīṭukaḷ oli vaṭivam; olivaṭivattiṉ kuṟiyīṭāka eḻuttu vaṭivam amikiṉṟatu.
ovvoru paṭiyilum moḻi iyaṟkiyāy vaḷarnta paṭimuṟikaḷi, ellāp paṭiyilum pintiya eḻuttu vaṭivap paṭiyē namakku naṉku cuṭṭik kāṭṭukiṟatu.
eḻuttu vaṭiviṉ vaḷarcci : ōviya eḻuttu
varivaṭivamitiyil iṉṟiya ulakiṉ taliciṟanta moḻikaḷ tamiḻum tamiḻ cārnta ēṉiya intiya moḻikaḷumē yākum. ivaṟṟil maṭṭumtāṉ ōr ōlikku ōr eḻuttu. ōr eḻuttukku ōr oli eṉṟa oli nūliṉ uyir kuṟikkōḷ amiti kiṭṭattaṭṭa muḻu aḷavil kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. attuṭaṉ ivaṟṟil maṭṭumē uyir oli, mey oli ākiya vakuppu muṟiyum, olip piṟappaṭippaṭiyāka avaṟṟiṉ varici muṟiyum amintuḷḷaṉa. ittaku tiṭṭaṅkaḷ mēli airōppiya moḻikaḷilō, naṭu nilak kaṭal cārnta epirēya, ekiptiya, iculāmiya moḻikaḷilōkūṭak kiṭiyātu.
ivvaḷavu ciṟanta varivaṭiva muṟi evvāṟu tōṉṟiṟṟu? evvāṟu vaḷarntatu? ulakap paṇpāṭu varalāṟukaḷil itu cuvi mikunta oru varalāṟṟuk kūṟu ākum.
eḻuttiṉ vaḷarccip paṭikaḷip paṇṭiya ekiptiya eḻuttu muṟikaḷ, paṇṭiya cīṉa eḻuttu muṟi, tol paḻaṅkālac cumēriya eḻuttumuṟi, cintu veḷi eḻuttu muṟi ākiyaviyē cuṭṭik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, ellāp paṭikaḷiyum viḷakkik kāṭṭuvatu ekiptiya muṟi oṉṟē.
oliyiṉ kuṟiyīṭākavum oli vaṭiviṉ kuṟiyīṭṭut tokutiyākavum viḷaṅkuvatu iṉṟiya nākarika ulakiṉ eḻuttu muṟi. āṉāl, ulakiṉ toṭakkak kālattiya mutal eḻuttu muṟi oliyiṉ kuṟiyīṭākavō oli vaṭiviṉ kuṟiyīṭākavō amiyavilli. nēraṭiyākak karuttiṉ kuṟiyīṭākak kūṭa amiyavilli. karuttiyum kaṭantu poruḷiṉ kuṟiyīṭākap poruḷiṉ ōviya vaṭivamākavē atu tōṉṟiṟṟu. ekiptiṉ mikap paḻimiyāṉa eḻuttu muṟi itik kāṭṭukiṟatu. im mutaṟpaṭi vari vaṭivi nām ōviya eḻuttu vaṭivu eṉkiṟōm.
tamiḻakattil caṅkakālam vari inta ōviya eḻuttu muṟi kiviṭappaṭāmal nīṭittiruntatu. itaṉit tamiḻ ilakkiyam ‘kaṇṇeḻuttu’ eṉṟum, iti eḻutiyavarik ‘kaṇṇeḻuttāḷar’ eṉṟum kuṟittatu. cuṅkat tuṟiyil irāṇṭāyira āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ inta eḻuttē cuṅkak kuṟiyīṭāka vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu.
tamiḻil eḻututal eṉṟa viṉic col ilakkiya marapil iṉṟaḷavum eḻuttu eḻutuvatu, ōviyam tīṭṭuvatu, kaṇṇukku mi tīṭṭuvatu ākiya ceyalkaḷi oruṅkē kuṟippatu kāṇalām. itaṉaṭippaṭiyāka aminta ōr uvami tirukkuṟaḷilum kāṭciyaḷikkiṟatu.
``eḻutuṅkāl kōlkāṇāk kaṇṇēpōl, koṇkaṉ
paḻikāṇēṉ kaṇṭa viṭattu’’						(tiruk. 1285)
ōviya eḻuttu marapiṉ paṇpu
ōviya eḻuttu muṟiyil ‘kākki’ eṉpatik kākkiyiṉ paṭamum ‘mīṉ’ eṉpati mīṉiṉ paṭamum, ‘erutu’ eṉpati erutiṉ paṭamumē kuṟittaṉa. immuṟi nākarikam vaḷarāta kālattiya muṟiyē. āyiṉum, iṉṟum ituvē kalviyaṟi vaṟṟavarkkum kalvi kaṟṟavarkkum potuvāṉa orē muṟi yākavum, moḻikku moḻi potuvāṉa, moḻi cārāta orē muṟiyākavum amintuḷḷatu. ūrtikaḷilum tērtal peṭṭikaḷilum peyarkaḷiviṭa eṇṇum, eṇṇiviṭap paṭamum niṟamum kallātavarkaḷum uṇarum ciṟanta kuṟiyīṭukaḷāy amikiṉṟaṉa. attuṭaṉ tamiḻarum teluṅkarum, āṅkilēyarum cīṉarum vēṟupāṭiṉṟi avaṟṟi uṇarakkūṭum!
cīṉattiṉ paṇṭiya eḻuttumuṟi ōviya eḻuttup paṭiyiliruntu neṭuntoli cellavilli. aṇimi vari ilakkiya vaḻakkil ammuṟiyē iruntu vantuḷḷatu. itaṉāl cīṉattukkuc cila naṉmikaḷkūṭa ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. paṇṭiya cīṉa ilakkiya ēṭoṉṟip ‘pīkkiṅ’ nakarattārum, ‘kaṇṭōṉ’ nakarattārum vācippar. kēṭpavarkku iraṇṭum vēṟu vēṟu moḻiyāy ‘oli vaṭivil’ tōṉṟiṉālum, ‘varivaṭivil’ atu cīṉa makkaḷ ellārkkum orē poruḷuṭiya orē ilakkiya māy amintiruntatu.
cīṉamum olikkuṟiyīṭṭu eḻuttumuṟiyip piṉpaṟṟi iṉṟu nākarikattil muṉṉēṟṟam peṟṟu varukiṟatāṉālum, paḻiya muṟiyāl ēṟpaṭṭa oṟṟumi pēraḷavil keṭṭuḷḷatu eṉṉalām.
ekiptiyariṭiyē ōviya eḻuttu muṟi ki.mu. 3000-kkum ki.mu. 2000-kkum iṭiyē tōṉṟi ataṉpiṉ uruveḻut tākavum olik kuṟiyīṭṭeḻuttākavum vaḷarcciyaṭintatu.
ōviyap paṭiyilēyē, pala karuttukaḷil poruḷ kuṟiyīṭṭukku iṭamillā tiruntatu. nīrvēṭki, vīram mutaliya paṇpukaḷiyum ‘ceytāṉ,’ ‘kuṭittāṉ’ mutaliya viṉi kaḷiyum nērākap paṭam piṭikka muṭiyātu. nākki nīṭṭi nīrttuḷi kāṭṭuvatāl nīr vēṭkiyum, vēli uyarttik kāṭṭuvatāl vīramum, kiyit taṉiyē kāṭṭuvatāl ceyalum, ūmi iṉṟum ciki kāṭṭuvatupōl peruvirali vāyil vittuk kāṭṭuvatāl kuṭittalum kuṟiyīṭukaḷākak kāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. pukaḻ oḷiyik katiravaṉoḷiyākak kāṭṭuvatu ivvakiyil mika uyarnta kuṟiyīṭṭu marapup paṇpu ākum.
ōviya eḻuttuk kālattiṉ kuṟiyīṭṭu marapē tamiḻakattiṉ paratanāṭṭiya, nāṭaka marapukaḷāka vaḷarntaṉa eṉṉalām.


uruveḻuttum olik kuṟiyīṭum
ōviya vaṭivit teḷivāka variyāmal, ciṟu kuḻantikaḷ kuṟittuk kāṭṭuvatupōla oṉṟiraṇṭu varikaḷāl kuṟittuk kāṭṭuvatē uru eḻuttu ākum. intap paṭiyil poruḷukkum varivaṭivukkum uḷḷa toṭarpu paṭippaṭiyākat tēyvuṟṟatu. ‘mīṉ’ kuṟiyīṭu iraṇṭu vaḷikōṭukaḷ āyiṟṟu. kākkik kuṟiyīṭu uṭal kuṟitta cāy variyum kāl kuṟitta iraṇṭu kuṟu vakikaḷum āyiṟṟu.
mīṉ paṭattiyum, kākkip paṭattiyum yārum payiṟci yillāmalē kaṇṭavuṭaṉ mīṉ eṉṟum kākki eṉṟum vācittuviṭa muṭiyum. āṉāl, avaṟṟiṉ uruvarikaḷip payiṟciyaṟṟavar vācikka muṭiyātu.
iṉṟiya ulakil āṅkila eḻuttup payiṟciyaṟṟavaṉ āṅkilat tiyō, tamiḻ eḻuttup payiṟciyaṟṟavaṉ tamiḻiyō paṭikka muṭiyātu. inta ikkāla moḻi niliyi uru eḻuttukaḷ aṭintu viṭṭaṉa.
paṇpuk kuṟiyīṭukaḷil kuṟiyīṭu poruḷiliruntu iṉṉum māṟupaṭṭatu. inniliyil kuṟiyīṭukaḷ poruḷkaḷ cuṭṭupavi eṉpatu maṟakkappaṭṭatu. colli atāvatu colliṉ olivaṭivic cuṭṭuvatāka māṟupaṭṭatu.
poruḷiṉ kuṟiyīṭu colliṉ kuṟiyīṭāṉatu iṉṟu oru periya cīrtiruttamākat tōṉṟātirukkakkūṭum. āṉāl, moḻi vaḷarcciyil aḵtu oru perum puraṭci ākum. ēṉeṉil, itaṉāl ‘eru’, ‘mi’ eṉṟa iru poruḷkaḷiṉ kuṟiyīṭukaḷē, ‘eru’ eṉṟa iru eḻuttukaḷiyum, ‘mi’ eṉṟa eḻuttiyum kuṟitta kuṟiyīṭāy, iṇintu ‘erumi’ eṉpataṉ iṇik kuṟiyīṭāka muṭintatu! ‘mā’, ‘kāy’ eṉṟa kuṟiyīṭukaḷ itupōlavē ‘māṅkāy’ eṉṟa colliṉ iṇik kuṟiyīṭāka muṭintatu!
coṟ kuṟiyīṭukaḷ nāḷaṭivil acik kuṟiyīṭākavum āyiṟṟu. atāvatu ‘ammi’ pōṉṟa col ‘am’ eṉṟa mutalaciyiyum piṉ ‘a’ eṉṟa mutaleḻuttiyum kuṟikkat toṭaṅkiṟṟu. mīṉ eṉṟa colliṉ kuṟiyīṭu `mī’ eṉṟa eḻuttiṉ kuṟiyīṭāyiṟṟu.
mokañcatarōviluḷḷa cintuveḷi eḻuttukaḷ mutaleḻuttuk kuṟiyīṭṭu niliyi ki.mu. 3000-ttilēyē aṭintiruntaṉa. ekiptiya eḻuttukaḷukkum cumēriya eḻuttukaḷukkum avi muṟpaṭṭa vaḷarcci peṟṟiruntaṉa. avaṟṟukkuc cintu veḷi eḻuttukaḷē vaḻi kāṭṭikaḷā yiruntaṉa eṉpati itu kāṭṭukiṟatu. cīṉa eḻuttukaḷukkum aviyē tūṇṭutalāy iruntirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu karuta iṭamuṇṭu. ēṉeṉil, nākarika ulakil āriyar varavāl ēṟpaṭṭa kuḻappam kāraṇamāka, ulakat toṭarpukaḷ aḻintu cīṉam tuṇṭupaṭṭapiṉ, cīṉa eḻuttu muṟi vaḷarcciyaṟṟu niṉṟuviṭṭatu.
tamiḻ eḻuttukaḷum ēṉiya ellā inti eḻuttukaḷum camaṟkiruta eḻuttukaḷum cintuveḷi eḻuttiṉ tiripu vaḷarcci vakikaḷē. āṉāl, tamiḻ eḻuttukaḷē cintu veḷi eḻuttukaḷiṉ nērmarapil vantavi. iṉṟiya tamiḻ eḻuttukaḷip pōlavē aviyum muppatāy iruntaṉa. eḻuttu muṟimi, vakuppu, varivaṭivu ākiya mūṉṟilum tamiḻ eḻuttolikaḷi mūla olikaḷākak koṇṭē camaṟkirutam mutaliya ēṉiya intiya moḻikaḷiṉ eḻuttukaḷ vakukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu kāṇṭal eḷitu.
tamiḻ, epirēya, arapu moḻikaḷ
cīṉamarapu, tamiḻmarapu, mēli marapu ākiya ulakiṉ mūṉṟu eḻuttu marapukaḷuḷ, cīṉa marapu olik kuṟiyīṭṭu vaḷarcciyi eṉṟum ceṉṟeṭṭa villi. col kuṟiyīṭu allatu acik kuṟiyīṭākavē niṉṟatu. maṟṟa iraṇṭu marapukaḷum itaṉ piṉṉum pēraḷavil potuvaḷarcciyuṭiyavi. ip potumarapup paṇpit tamiḻ marapu cārnta intiya moḻikaḷ, oru puṟamum mēli marapiṉ paṇṭimoḻikaḷāṉa arapu, epirēya moḻikaḷ maṟṟoru puṟamum kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. arapu, epirēya moḻikaḷ uṇmiyil paḻimiyit tamiḻmarapu allatu intiya marapiviṭa muṉippākavē cuṭṭik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.
tamiḻ ilakkaṇattil eḻuttukaḷ allatu olikaḷil mutaṉmiyiṭam uyirkaḷukkē tarappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ‘uyir’ eṉṟa peyarē itic cuṭṭik kāṭṭukiṟatu. uyirkaḷ tāmē iyaṅkupavi, meykaḷ uṭal pōṉṟavi. uyir cārntē iyaṅkupavi eṉṟu tamiḻ ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ viḷakkukiṉṟaṉa. nākarika ulakiṉ perumpālāṉa moḻikaḷ ikkarutti iṉṟu ēṟṟuk koṇṭuḷḷaṉa. āyiṉum, iḵtu olivaṭivu paṟṟi eḻunta tamiḻar ārāycciyiṉ payaṉē. varivaṭiva aṭippaṭiyāka eḻunta karutti arapu, epirēya moḻi ilakkaṇa aṟiñar karuttum, ammoḻikaḷiṉ varivaṭivamum naṉku kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻ marapilkūṭa akkaruttiṉ cuvaṭu illāmalilli.
arapu, epirēya ilakkaṇa nūlār karuttuppaṭi, olikaḷuḷ mutaṉmi vāyntavi uyirkaḷalla; meykaḷē. taṉittiyaṅku paviyum uyirkaḷalla, meykaḷē eṉṟu koḷḷappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. meykaḷi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭē uyirkaḷ iyaṅkukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu avarkaḷ karutiyuḷḷaṉar.
mēlīṭākap pārppavarkaḷukkum, arapu, epirēya ilakkaṇa marapil payilātavarkaḷukkum itu poruntāk kūṟṟu eṉṟē tōṉṟum. āṉāl, tamiḻ mutaliya moḻikaḷilēyē taṉi meykaḷi ik, iṅ eṉṟō, ka, ṅa eṉṟō ku, ṅu eṉṟōtāṉ olikka muṭiyumēyaṉṟit taṉittu olikka muṭiyātu. atumaṭṭumaṉṟu, i, e mutaliya uyir olikaḷi yi, ye eṉṟa uyir meykaḷ pōṉṟum, u, o mutaliya uyir olikaḷi vu, vo eṉṟa uyir meykaḷ (allatu āṅkila muṟippaṭi (ṟara,ṟaṭi) pōṉṟumē nām olikkiṟōm. teluṅkar eḻuttu marapilēyē inta iyaṟki māṟupāṭṭik kāṇalām. avarkaḷ uppu eṉpati vuppu eṉṟum illu eṉpati yillu eṉṟumē eḻutukiṉṟaṉar.
ellā uyirkaḷumē y,v (ṟa) pōṉṟa ōr aṭippaṭi meyyiṉ utavikoṇṭē olippatāka arapu, epirēya ilakkaṇa nūlār koḷkiṉṟaṉar. uyiriṉ aṭippaṭi mūla oli eṉṟu kūṟattakka inta oliyē eḻuttukaḷuḷ mutaleḻuttāka ammoḻikaḷil kāṇappaṭum arapu ‘aliḵv’ allatu epirēya ‘ālḵvā’ eḻuttu ākum. inta aṭippaṭi mey (aliḵv) eḻuttuṭaṉ akara vuyir cēra akara oliyum, ākāra, ikara mutaliya uyirkaḷ cēra ākāra ikaram mutaliya olikaḷum eḻukiṉṟaṉa.
ellā olikaḷum ‘uyir mey’ olikaḷē
paṇṭi ulaka eḻuttukaḷ ellāmē uyir mey eḻuttukaḷākak karutappaṭṭiruntaṉa eṉpati itu kāṭṭukiṟatu. ‘kāṭu’ eṉṟa col ‘kā’ eṉṟa mutaleḻuttiyum ‘āṭu’ eṉṟa col ‘ā’ eṉṟa mutaleḻuttiyum kuṟittapōtu, ‘kā’ ‘ā’ eṉṟa iru olikaḷum oru taṉmiyuṭiyavi (atāvatu uyir meykaḷ) eṉṟē karutappaṭṭiruntaṉa eṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu. itu pōlavē ‘kīri’yiliruntu ‘kī’yum ‘īṭṭi’yiliruntu ‘īyum tōṟṟiyapiṉtāṉ ‘kā-kī’ toṭarpum, ā-ī toṭarpum veḷippaṭṭaṉavākalām. itaṉ payaṉāka, ‘ka’ mutal ku’ variyuḷḷa eḻuttukaḷ orē meyt toṭarpuṭiya uyir meykaḷāka vakukkappaṭṭu, ‘ka’ eṉṟa aṭippaṭi vaṭiviṉ tiripukaḷāka avi amintaṉa. āṉāl, itē camayam ‘ka’ mutal ku’ variyuḷḷa tokuti pōlavē ‘a’ mutal oḷa’ variyuḷḷa tokutiyum karutappaṭṭaṉa. ‘ka-ku’ tokuti uyirillāta ‘k’ eṉṟa (mey oli) aṭippaṭi oliyiliruntu uruvāyiṟṟu. ati uyir meykaḷiṉ aṭippaṭi oliyāṉa ‘mey’ ākki neṭuṅkaṇakkil iṟutiyil cērttaṉar. itupōlavē ‘a-oḷa’ tokuti muṭivilum tamiḻil ḵ eṉṟa vari vaṭivu ēṟpaṭṭatu. arapu-epirēya moḻikaḷil ‘aliḵv’ eṉṟa eḻuttu ittakiyatē. oliyiyal muṟiyil ‘k’ eṉṟa eḻuttu ‘ka-ku’ tokutikku mūlamāyiruntatupōl, ‘a-oḷa’ tokutikku āytam (ḵ) allatu aliḵv mūlamākak karutappaṭṭatu. 
arapu-epirēya moḻiyil ‘aliḵv’ uyiralla, mey. āṉāl, kirēkkar inta eḻutti ‘ālḵvā’ eṉṟa mutaleḻuttākki ati uyirākavē koṇṭaṉar. āṉāl, ‘aliḵv’ eṉṟa mey valloliyāṉa ōr āytamākavum (vaṭamoḻi h), melloliyāṉa ōr āytamākavum (arapu-epirēya āliḵv) iru kuṟiyīṭukaḷāka amintaṉa sakirēkkariṉ vallāytam ḷuyīciviraḷa yaḷayīciyavaraḷa (‘), mellāytam ḷuyīciviraḷa ṭunanḷi (’)]
tamiḻ āytam vaṭamoḻi valloliyiviṭa valimi yuṭiyatāka iṉṟu olikkappaṭukiṟatu. āṉāl, tolkāppiyar kālattil atu mey olikaḷukku uyirppaṇpu allatu aṅkāppup paṇpūṭṭum oru cārpuk kuṟiyīṭākavē koḷḷap paṭṭatu.
uyirmeyt toṭarpu
uyirillāmal uṭampu iyaṅkātu eṉṟa tamiḻ ilakkaṇa nūlār karuttu uṇmiyiṉ oru pakuti-ōr ari uṇmiyē yākum. arapu-epirēya ilakkaṇa nūlār kūṟṟu ataṉ maṟṟoru pātiyi, maṟṟōr ari uṇmiyit terivikkiṟatu. uṭalillāmal uyir iyaṅkātu eṉpatē atu.
ulakil uyirillāmal uṭal uṇṭu. āṉāl, aḵtu iyaṅkāta uṭal. atip piṇam eṉpōm. nērmāṟāka, uṭal illāta uyir eṅkum kiṭikkavē kiṭiyātu. aḵtu uṇmiyil maṉitaṉ kaṟpaṉi maṭṭumē. ākavē, ulakil ‘uyir-uṭal’ iyinta oru poruḷtāṉ meyyāka iyalkiṟatu.
itupōlavē olikaḷ ellāmē meymmiyil ‘uyir-mey’ olikaḷē. ‘mey’ olikaḷiyum ‘uyir’ olikaḷiyum maṉitaṉ nuṇṇaṟivārāycciyāl meyyārnta kaṟpaṉi vaṭivukaḷākavē pirittuṇarkiṟāṉ. ‘kā’ pōṉṟa ‘meyyiṉ piṉ uyir iṇivu’ pōlavē ‘āl’ eṉpatil ‘uyiriṉ piṉ mey iṇivum’ ‘kāl’ eṉpatil ‘meyyiṉ piṉ uyirum avaṟṟiṉ piṉ meyyum’ (mey-uyir-mey āka) iṇintuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟic cērttu olikka muṭiyum. pirittuṇaravum muṭivum. āṉāl, taṉittaṉi olikaḷāka olikka muṭiyātu.
mēli marapil kirēkkarum kīḻi marapil tamiḻarum maṭṭumē uyirkaḷit taṉi olikaḷākak kaṇṭaṉar; kaṇṭu vakuttaṉar. immuṟi mēli marapil paṇṭi moḻikaḷil kiṭiyātu. iṉṟiya iculāmiya moḻikaḷilum illi. itupōlavē mey olikaḷit taṉiyākak karutiya mutaliṉaṅkaḷum tamiḻarum kirēkkarumē. āṉāl, iṅkē tamiḻarum kirēkkarum varivaṭivil vakuttuk koṇṭa muṟi vēṟupaṭṭatu. kirēkkar paḻiya ‘uyir mey’ mūlavaṭiviyē mey ākkiṉar. tamiḻar paḻiya ‘uyirmey’ mūlavaṭivi akaram cārnta ‘uyirmey’ vaṭivākak koṇṭu, atiliruntu mey oliyi mēlē puḷḷiyiṭṭu varuvittukkoṇṭaṉar. iṅkē tamiḻartām varivaṭivil piṟpaṭṭuviṭṭaṉar eṉpatil aiyamilli. ēṉeṉil, tamiḻar muṟi epirēya, arapu varivaṭivukaḷiṉ muṟiyē. avi kaṭanta vaḷarcciyalla.
vaḷarcci kuṉṟiya ittamiḻar muṟiyē iṉṟiya ellā intiya eḻuttukaḷiṉ kīḻi ulaka marapākavum, kirēkkariṉ tiruntiya muṟiyē ellā airōppiya eḻuttukaḷiṉ mēli marapukaḷākavum amintaṉa. varivaṭivelli yil inta oru kūṟṟil maṭṭum kīḻināṭu piṟpaṭṭu mēli nāṭukaḷ muṟpaṭṭuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu kūṟalām. āyiṉum, immuṟpōkkukkuk kirēkkarē muṟṟilum kāraṇarāvar eṉpatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu.
tamiḻar marapiṉ vaḷarcci varivaṭiviltāṉ iṅṅaṉam taṭippaṭṭatu. olivaṭivārāycciyil avarkaḷ kirēkkariyum mēli ulakiyum tāṇṭi muṉṉēṟiviṭṭaṉar. oliyiyal muṟippaṭi uyirkaḷiyum, meykaḷiyum, uyirkku mūla oliyāṉa āytattiyum maṭṭumē taṉi olikaḷākki, uyirmeykaḷik kūṭṭolikaḷāka vakutta paṇpil avarkaḷ ulakiṉ maṟṟa moḻiyiṉaṅkaḷaṉittiyum mūvāyira āṇṭukaṭku muṉpē viñciviṭṭaṉar. ēṉeṉil, iṉṟiya intiya moḻikaḷiṉ varivaṭiṅkaḷaṉittum-camaṟkirutam uṭpaṭa-tamiḻmarapu kaṭantu vaḷarcci peṟāmal atiyē vaḷiya vaḷiyap piṉpaṟṟi niṟpatu kāṇalām. pala kūṟukaḷil atu tamiḻ marapilēkūṭak kuṟipaṭukiṟatu. kīḻ varuvaṉa itaṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷākum.
1. 	uyirolikaḷuḷ meypōṉṟa uyir eṉṟa muṟiyil āytam tamiḻil uyiriṉ piṉvikkappaṭṭatu. inniliyil uḷḷa iraṇṭu eḻuttukaḷ (aṉu°vāram, vicarkkam ākiyaṉa) camaṟkirutattilum appaṭiyē. āṉāl, uyir niliyuṭiya meykaḷākiya nāṉku olikaḷ (ru, rū, lu, lū.) iṭiyē ūkārattukkum ēkārattukkum naṭuvil vikkappaṭṭaṉa. ivi kuṟṟiyalukaram pōṉṟaṉa eṉṟa eṇṇamē ivvarici muṟiyiṉ kāraṇamākalām.
2. 	tamiḻil vali, meli, iṭi eṉpaṉa āṟu āṟāka amintuḷḷaṉa. itupōla camaṟkirutattil aintintāka amikkum muyaṟci ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷatu. itaṟkāka iṭiccol nāṉkuṭaṉ cīṟolikaḷ mūṉṟaṉuḷ oṉṟu (ḵcya) uṭaṉ tarappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ēṉiya iraṇṭu cīṟolikaḷum āytam pōṉṟa oli (ha) oṉṟum, iraṇṭu kūṭṭoli kaḷuṭaṉ (tra,kṣa) cērttu aintākap piṉṉi vikkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
3. 	oli vaṭiviliruntu vanta varaṉmuṟiyiṉ iyalpukku māṟāka, iṭivaru meykaḷ piṉvarum uyir meykaḷuṭaṉ cērttuk kūṭṭeḻuttukaḷāka vari vaṭivilē koḷḷap paṭukiṉṟaṉa.
eṇmāṉam
eḻuttukaḷ olivaṭiviṉ kuṟiyīṭukaḷ. āṉāl, moḻiyil kuṟiyīṭṭu muṟimi ittuṭaṉ niṟkavilli. eḻuttut tokutiyiṉ maṟṟoru paṭik kuṟiyīṭṭu muṟi ulakil ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷatu. ituvē eṇ muṟi allatu eṇmāṉam. oṉṟu, iraṇṭu eṉṟu eḻuttil eppōtum eḻutuvatu vāyppu vaḷamuṭiyatalla. eḻuttiviṭa eṇkaḷukkuc curukkak kuṟiyīṭu tēvip paṭṭatu. 1,2,3 eṉṟa kuṟiyīṭukaḷ itaṉāl eḻuntaṉa. ētō oru mūlamoḻiyil oṉṟu, iraṇṭu eṉṟa eṇṇup peyarkaḷiṉ mutaleḻuttuk kuṟiyīṭṭiṉ tēyvākavē avi tōṟṟiyirukkakkūṭum eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar cilar karutiyuḷḷaṉar. am mūlamoḻi etu eṉpati nām iṉṟum uṟutiyiṭṭuk kāṇa muṭiyavilli. āyiṉum, mūlamoḻikku aṇimiyiṉam allatu aṇimiyiṉaṅkaḷ yāvi eṉṟu uṇara muṭikiṟatu.
am mūlamoḻiyiṉam vāṇikam, vāṉa nūl mutaliya aṟivut tuṟikaḷit taṉ vāḻviṉ uyir niliyākak koṇṭa moḻiyiṉamāka iruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatil aiyamilli. ēṉeṉil, eḻuttiviṭa mikutiyāka eṇ kuṟiyīṭu tēvip paṭṭatu itaṉālēyē.
eṇkaḷi nām oṉṟu mutal pattuvaritāṉ taṉic collākak kuṟikkiṟōm. ataṟku mēl patiṉoṉṟu (pattuṭaṉ oṉṟu) paṉṉiraṇṭu (pattuṭaṉ iraṇṭu) eṉṟum, pattuṭaṉ pattaṭutta piṉ irupatu (iraṇṭu pattu), muppatu (mūṉṟu pattu) eṉṟum pattaṭukkiya patti (pattup patti) nūṟu eṉṟum kuṟiyīṭṭup peyarākki mēṟceṉṟeṇṇukiṟōm. immuṟi pattaṭukkiya muṟi allatu patiṉmāṉa muṟi (ṉunuiayaṭa ḷulaḷavanaa) eṉappaṭum.
varivaṭivil oṉṟu mutal pattuvari oru taṉik kuṟiyīṭāy amiya villi. oṉṟu mutal oṉpatu variyē avvāṟu amintuḷḷatu. patteṉṉum tāṉatti oru cuṉṉamum ‘oru’ pattu eṉṉum ataṉ perukkatti iṭatu puṟam oṉṟu (1) eṉṟa kuṟiyum kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. itupōla irupatu, muppatu pōṉṟa pattiṉ perukkaṅkaḷit tāṉak kuṟiyīṭāṉa cuṉṉattukku muṉ oṉṟu iraṇṭu eṉṟa perukka eṇ kuṟiyiṭṭut terivikkiṟōm. nūṟu, āyiram eṉṟa pattaṭukkukaḷit tāṉa aṭukkukaḷiṉ eṇṇaḷavāṉa cuṉṉaṅkaḷiṭṭup perukka eṇkaḷuṭaṉ kuṟikkiṟōm. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka nūṟu eṉpatu pattu iraṇṭaṭukkiya iraṇṭāvatu tāṉa mātalāl iraṇṭu cuṉṉamum āyiram eṉpatu pattu mūṉṟaṭukkiya mūṉṟāvatu tāṉamātalāl mūṉṟu cuṉṉamum peṟukiṉṟaṉa.
patiṉ perukkamum, pattaṭukkum kaṭanta nūṟṟeṭṭu nūṟṟu irupatu pōṉṟa eṇ tokikaḷi, eṇkaḷiyum perukka eṇkaḷiyum cuṉṉattiṭamāka avvattāṉattiliṭṭu nirappukiṟōm.
patiṉaṭukku muṟiyiṉ varivaṭivam itu.
patiṉaṭukku muṟikku ivvāṟu oru karuttu vaṭivam vēṟākavum, varivaṭivam vēṟākavum amintuḷḷatu. palar inta iraṇṭu vaṭivaṅkaḷiyum oṉṟākak karutiviṭukiṟārkaḷ. itu tavaṟu. ēṉeṉṟāl karuttu vaṭivattilum oli vaṭivattilum patiṉaṭukku muṟi uṭiya moḻikaḷ varivaṭivattil ataṉi ēṟkātiruppatiyum, varivaṭivil ataṉi ēṟṟa moḻikaḷil ataṟku muṟpaṭṭa olivaṭivam nilavuvatiyum kāṇalām.
varivaṭivam, karuttu vaṭivam iraṇṭilum patiṉmuṟiyi ēṟkāta pala iṉaṅkaḷ ulakil iruntatuṇṭu. piṉṉāḷkaḷil aviyum patiṉ muṟiyi ēṟṟamintālum muṉṉiya muṟiyiṉ taṭaṅkaḷ avaṟṟuṭ cilamoḻikaḷil uḷḷaṉa. mālattīviṉ moḻiyilum āṅkilattilumkūṭap paṉṉiraṇṭiṉ muṟiyiṉ taṭaṅkal uḷḷaṉa. āṅkilattil paṉṉiraṇṭukku iṉṟu taṉiccol (ṭuvelvvkūṟanaṭena) iruttal kāṇalām. nūṟṟirupatukku oru colvaḻakkum (vaṟanaṭakavala) paṉṉiraṇṭu paṉṉiraṇṭukku (144 pacaṭiḷaḷa) oru tokutiyum uṇṭu.
ulaka ilakkamuṟi paṟṟiya kōṭpāṭukaḷ
iṉṟu ulaka muḻuvatum vaḻaṅkum patiṉmuṟiyiṉ varivaṭivam mēṉāṭṭiṉarāl arāpiya eṇ muṟi (hacayabiu nṭivayaviṭin) eṉak kuṟikkappaṭukiṟatu. itil oṉṟu mutal oṉpatu vari ilakkaṅkaḷum (1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 9) tāṉak kuṟiyīṭāka oru cuṉṉamum (0) irukkiṉṟaṉa. ārāycciyāḷar iṉṟu itaṉi ‘intiya eṇmuṟi’ eṉṟu tiruttamākak kuṟikkak karutukiṉṟaṉar. ēṉeṉṟāl, inta eṇ muṟiyi intiyariṭamiruntu mēṟkoṇṭatāka arāpiyarē kuṟittuḷḷaṉar. cuṉṉattuṭaṉ kūṭiya patiṉmāṉattiṉ inta varivaṭivatti vaḻaṅkiya mutal ulaka iṉam intiyāviluḷḷa camaṟkirutam vaḻaṅkiya iṉamē eṉṟum karutukiṟārkaḷ. cilar varivaṭiva ilakkaṅkaḷ camaṟkirutattiṉ ilakkap peyariṉ mutal eḻuttukaḷiṉ tiripē eṉṟukūṭak karutiyuḷḷārkaḷ.




tamiḻp pērāciriyarkaḷ karuttu
ulaka eṇ (arāpi allatu intiya eṇ) tamiḻ eṇkaḷiṉ tiripē eṉṟum, avaṟṟiṉ kuṟiyīṭukaḷ tamiḻ ilakkap peyarkaḷiṉ mutaleḻuttukaḷiṉ tiripē eṉṟum karutupavar uṇṭu. oppumikkāka ṭākṭar mu.varatarācaṉ avarkaḷiṉ moḻi varalāṟu pakkam 444-l kaṇṭa paṭṭiyali aṭip paṭiyākak koṇṭu, ikkarutti viḷakkukiṟōm. 
ilakkaṅkaḷiṉ mūla mutal moḻi etuvāyiṉum, atu camaṟkirutam eṉpataṉi valiyuṟuttavum, tamiḻ eṉpataṉi valiyuṟuttavum kāṭṭappaṭṭa viḷakkaṅkaḷ, varalāṟṟu muṟiyil teḷivuṭiyavi alla. ēṉeṉṟāl, iṉṟiya tamiḻiṉ eḻuttu vaṭivamō camaṟkirutattiṉ eḻuttu vaṭivamō ilakka vaṭivaṅkaḷōkūṭa avaṟṟiṉ tol paḻimi vaṭivaṅkaḷalla; ikkāla accu vaṭivaṅkaḷē. avaṟṟukku muṟpaṭṭa vaṭiva māṟutalkaḷ mutaleḻuttuṭaṉ ilakka vaṭivattukkuḷḷa ivvoppumikaḷip poruḷaṟṟavi ākki viṭukiṉṟaṉa. vāṉattu mukiliṉaṅkaḷi nōkki itu yāṉi, itu kutiri eṉa eṇṇuvatu pōla, ārāycciyāḷar ārvak kaṟpaṉikaḷi ittakiya viḷakkaṅkaḷ kuṟittuk kāṭṭiviṭattakkaviyāy uḷḷaṉa.
eḻuttu vaṭiviṉ mūlamoḻiyi nām cintuveḷiyil kāṇkiṟōm. eṇ vaṭivamum ammoḻiyilēyē toṭaṅkiṟṟu eṉṟu potumuṟiyāka uyttuṇarvatil tavaṟilli.
varalāṟṟu muṟiyāṉa pala iyalvātaṅkaḷ iti nōkkiyaviyāy uḷḷaṉa.
patiṉmāṉa vaḷarcci
iṉṟu arapu ilakkam allatu intiya ilakkam eṉṟu kuṟikkappaṭum ulaka ilakkamē kiṭṭattaṭṭa ellā moḻikkum potuvāka, ōviya eḻuttuppōla moḻi cārā ulakap potuk kuṟiyīṭāka vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. itu tavira, itaṉuṭaṉ toṭarpuṭiyatākavō, toṭarpaṟṟatākavō pala moḻikaḷilum vēṟu vēṟu ilakka muṟikaḷ nilavivantuḷḷaṉa.
eḻuttiṉ varivaṭiva vaḷarcciyiviṭa, ilakkattiṉ varivaṭiva vaḷarcci mikavum piṟpaṭṭatu eṉpatil aiyamilli. āṉāl, eḻuttiṉ varivaṭivil ekiptup pala toṭakkap paṭikaḷit toṭarcciyākak kāṭṭukiṟatu. ilakka vakiyil paṭikaḷi orē toṭarpāka enta iṉamum kāṭṭavilli. āyiṉum, ulakil vaḻakkilirunta varivaṭiva ilakka muṟikaḷi ellām oppiṭṭup pārttāl, avaṟṟiṉ vaḷarccip paṭi muṟikaḷiyum tōṟṟattiyum oruvāṟu uyttuṇaralām.
ilakka varivaṭivaṅkaḷi avaṟṟiṉ paṇpum paṭiyum nōkki nāṉku marapukaḷāka vakukkalām. oṉṟu arapu eṇ eṉṟu vaḻaṅkum putiya arapu muṟikku muṟpaṭṭa paḻiya arapu ilakka muṟiyum, kirēkka ilakka muṟiyum ākum. ituvē naṭunilak kaṭalakattiṉ paḻiya muṟi ākum. maṟṟoṉṟu urōma muṟi. mūṉṟāvatu tamiḻ ilakka muṟi. itaṉi aṭuttu vaḷarcciyaṭinta camaṟkirutattiṉ tēvanākariyilum, putiya arāpiya vaḻakkilum taṟkāla mēli ulakilum paranta muṟiyē ulaka ilakkamuṟi eṉṟu nām vaḻaṅkuvatu.








ivvellā ilakka muṟiyiṉ varivaṭivap paṇpu kaḷiyum kīḻēyuḷḷa aṭṭavaṇiyil oppiṭṭuk kāṇalām.
kirēkka, naṭunilak kaṭalaka marapu
kirēkka ilakkamuṟiyiṉ paṇpu muṟṟilum paḻiya arapu ilakka muṟiyi ottatē. mēli ulakil eḻuttu muṟiyil putuvatāka uyir olikaḷit taṉi eḻuttākak kuṟitta pukaḻiṉam kirēkka iṉam. āṉāl, varivaṭivil atu naṭunilak kaṭalaka marapi appaṭiyē mēṟkoṇṭatākat terikiṟatu. ataṉiṉṟum oru ciṟitum muṉṉēṟavilli.
naṭunilak kaṭalaka, kirēkka ilakka marapukaḷ varivaṭivil patiṉmāṉa muṟiyalla. āyiṉum, olivaṭivil avi patiṉmāṉa muṟiyi muḻutum eṭṭiyiruntaṉa. avaṟṟukku ammoḻikaḷil patiṉmāṉa muṟiyil coṟkaḷ amintiruntatu maṭṭumalla; varivaṭittilum pattuk kaṭanta eṇkaḷ (11, 12 mutaliyaṉa), pattiṉ aṭukkuk kaṭanta eṇkaḷ (21, 22, 31, 32 pōlvaṉa), nūṟu nūṟṟaṭukkuk kaṭanta eṇkaḷ (101, 102, 114, 142, 201, 202 pōlvaṉa) ākiyavaṟṟukkut taṉi ilakkam illi. kūṭṭu ilakkak kuṟiyīṭukaḷē (10+1, 20+1, 100+1, 100+10+1 pōlvaṉa) vaḻaṅkiṉa. kūṭṭu eṇkaḷil ilakkaṅkaḷiṭiyē kūṭṭal karuttē tokki niṉṟatu. atāvatu pattiṉ kuṟiyuṭaṉ oṉṟiṉ kuṟiyīṭṭāl atu patiṉoṉṟeṉṟu uṇarappaṭṭatu.
immuṟiyiṉ varivaṭivattiluḷḷa peruṅkuṟipāṭu pattaṭukkukaḷāṉa irupatu, muppatu mutaliyavaṟṟukkum nūṟṟaṭukkukaḷāṉa irunūṟu, munnūṟu mutaliyavaṟṟukkum taṉikkuṟiyīṭukaḷ amintaṉa.
ilakkak kuṟiyīṭukaḷ teḷivāṉa taṉikkuṟiyīṭukaḷalla. neṭuṅ kaṇakkiṉ eḻuttukaḷē eṇmāṉak kuṟikaḷākavum koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. iḵtu immuṟiyiṉ maṟṟoru kuṟipāṭu ākum.
immuṟikaḷil 1457 eṉpati ilakkamākak kuṟikka vēṇṭumāṉāl, āyirattiṉ kuṟiyīṭu nānūṟṟiṉ kuṟiyīṭu, aimpatiṉ kuṟiyīṭu, ēḻiṉ kuṟiyīṭu iṭalām. 2000, 3000 mutaliya ilakkaṅkaḷi eḻuta āyirak kuṟiyīṭṭi iraṇṭu taṭavi, mūṉṟu taṭavi eḻutikkoṇṭētāṉ pōtal vēṇṭum. ēṉeṉṟāl, iraṇṭu pattu, iraṇṭu nūṟu, iraṇṭu āyiram eṉpavaṟṟil ciṟu eṇṇukkum peru eṇṇukkum iṭiyēyuḷḷa perukkaṟ kuṟippu aṉṟu uṇarappaṭṭatākat teriyavilli.
kirēkka, arapu muṟikaḷ ulakeṅkum parantirunta muṟikaḷ eṉpatil aiyamilli. tamiḻilum camaṟkirutattilum ataṉ taṭaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. uyireḻuttukaḷukkō, mey eḻuttu kaḷukkō oṉṟu mutal cuṉṉam vari eṇ matippuk koṭuttu, ilakkaṅkaḷi vēṇṭiya collākkum muṟi iru moḻikaḷilum uṇṭu. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka a, ā, i, ī, u, ū, e, ē, ai, o, ō ākiyavaṟṟukkum muṟiyē 1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8,9,0 eṉṟa matippukkoṭuttāl tiruvāviṉaṉkuṭi eṉṟa col 35, 23, 153 (muppattintu ilakkattu irupattu mūṉṟāyirattu nūṟṟimpattu mūṉṟu) ākiṟatu. itu iṉṟu kirēkkarippōla eṇ kūṭṭu muṟiyip piṉpaṟṟāmal, patiṉmāṉattiṉ tāṉamuṟiyiyē piṉpaṟṟukiṟatu. āyiṉum, patiṉmāṉa muṟikku muṉṉirunta eṇ kūṭṭu muṟiyiṉ marapil ivvaḻakku ēṟpaṭṭatu eṉṟu kūṟa iṭamuṇṭu.
pāracīkarum arāpiyarum eḻuttu - eṇṇil iti eṇkūṭṭu muṟiyākavē kavitiyil tiṟampaṭa vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷaṉar.
urōma ilakka marapu
patiṉmāṉattiṉ varivaṭiva vaḷarcciyil urōma ilakka muṟi kirēkka, paḻiya arapu muṟikaḷiṉiṉṟum oru paṭi muṉṉēṟi yuḷḷatu. atē camayam atu ki viralkaḷi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu iyaṟkiyāka vaḷarntatākavum terikiṟatu.
oṉṟu mutal nāṉku variyuḷḷa ilakkaṅkaḷ viral kuṟikaḷē (I,II,III,IIII). aintu (V) nāṉku viralkaḷ cērttu oru puṟamum peru viral orupuṟamāka viritta kiyiṉ vaṭivam. pattu (X) aintuviralkaḷum cērttu oru kimēl oru ki kuṟukkākiya vaṭivam. inta viḷakkam ōr aḻakāṉa iyaṟki vaḷarcci kuṟitta viḷakkamē.
aimpatu (L),, nūṟu (C),, ainnūṟu (D), āyiram (M), āyivaṟṟiṉ kuṟikaḷ avvavaṟṟukkuriya ilattīṉ moḻic colliṉ mutaleḻutteṉpatu teḷivu. ituvum iyalpāṉa ōr aḻakiya muṉṉēṟṟak kuṟiyīṭē.
kirēkka, arapu muṟikaḷiviṭa iyalpāṉa inta muṟi avaṟṟiviṭap patiṉmāṉam nōkkiya muṉṉēṟṟamuṭiya muṟiyākavum amintuḷḷatu. pattaṭukkukaḷukkum (20, 30 mutaliyavaṟṟukkum) nūṟaṭukkukaḷukkum (200, 300 mutaliya vaṟṟukkum) taṉik kuṟiyīṭukaḷiviṭa aṭukkiya, pattup patiṉmāṉap paṭikaḷē ciṟappuṭiyaṉa (20=XX, 30=XXX, 200=CC, 300=CCC eṉṟavāṟu),
attuṭaṉ pēreṇṇiṉ piṉ ciṟṟeṇ varumpōtu kūṭṭaṟkuṟippum (VI=5+1=6, XI=10+1=11 eṉṟavāṟu), pēreṇṇiṉ muṉ ciṟṟeṇ varumpōtu kaḻittaṟkuṟippum (V=5-1=4, IX=10-1=9 eṉṟavāṟu) itil payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
maṟṟellā vakikaḷilum kirēkkari niḻalpōlap piṉpaṟṟiya urōmarkaḷ, ilakkavakiyil taṉi muṉṉēṟṟam aṭintuḷḷatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu. āṉāl, kirēkkar naṭunilak kaṭalaka iṉattiṉ kīḻttici yiluḷḷa oru marapip piṉpaṟṟiyatu pōlavē, atē iṉattiṉ oru mēltici marapi avarkaḷ piṉpaṟṟiṉar eṉṟum uyttuṇaralām. avarkaḷ ciṟpak kalikkut tūṇṭutal tanta aḻintupaṭṭa tolpaḻaṅkāla eṭra°kāṉa iṉattiṉ marapākavum aḵtu amital kūṭum.
urōma muṟiyiṉ kuṟipāṭukaḷum pala. oṉṟu mutal oṉpatu vari yuḷḷa aṭippaṭi ilakkaṅkaḷ aṉittukkum taṉik kuṟikaḷamiyāmal palavaṟṟukkum (VI, VII pōla) kūṭṭuk kuṟikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. pattip pōlavē aintukkum, nūṟṟippōla aimpatukkum, āyirattip pōla ainnūṟukkum taṉikkuṟikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. patiṉmāṉattiṉ tāṉamatippi ivi keṭukkiṉṟaṉa.
iṉṟiya patiṉmāṉamuṟi ulakeṅkum paranta piṉkūṭa, mēli ulakil, ataṟkaṭuttapaṭiyāka, tuṇivaṭivāka urōmamuṟi iṉṟum vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. ataṉ aṭippaṭit takutikkum, matippukkum iḵtu oru cāṉṟu ākum.
tamiḻ ilakka muṟi
tamiḻ ilakka muṟiyiṉ toṭakka vaḷarcciyiyō, camaṟkiruta tēvanākarika muṟiyiṉ toṭakka vaḷarcciyiyō nām kāṇamuṭiyavilli. āṉāl, tamiḻ ilakkamuṟi piṉṉataṉiviṭa orupaṭi muṟpaṭṭa vaḷarcciyik kāṭṭukiṟatu. ulakiṉ mutal patiṉmāṉa muṟi atuvē eṉṉalām.
kirēkka, arapu muṟikaḷiṉ paṭikaḷiyum, urōma muṟiyiṉ paṭiyiyum kaṭantē tamiḻ, tēva nākarika muṟikaḷ muṉṉēṟiṉa eṉṟu kūṟattaṭiyilli. āṉāl, maṟṟa muṟikaḷ toṭakkap paṭikaḷil irukkumpōtē ivi kiṭṭattaṭṭa muḻuniṟi patiṉmāṉa muṟikaḷāy iyaṅkiṉa. maṟṟavaṟṟiṉ paṭikaḷi ivi varalāṟṟuk keṭṭāta muṟpaṭṭa kālattilēyē kaṭantaṉa eṉṟum toli nāṭukaḷiluḷḷa toṭakkap paṭikaḷ iṅkiruntu paraviya toṭakkappaṭikaḷākavē irukkak kūṭumeṉṟum kūṟa iṭamuṇṭu.
kirēkka arapu muṟikaḷ tāṇṭiyum urōmamuṟi tāṇṭiyum tamiḻmuṟi patiṉmāṉattil pala paṭikaḷ muṉṉēṟiyuḷḷatu.
mutalāvatāka oṉṟu mutal oṉpatu vari ilakkaṅ kaḷukkut taṉikkuṟiyīṭukaḷ uḷḷaṉa.
ivi kaṭantu, pattu, nūṟu, āyiram ākiya tāṉaṅkaḷukkē teḷivāṉa taṉik kuṟiyīṭukaḷ uḷḷaṉa.
mūṉṟāvatāka, tāṉak kuṟiyīṭukaḷāka vaḻaṅkiya pēreṇkaḷukku muṉ ciṟṟeṇkaḷ avaṟṟiṉ maṭaṅkukaḷāka vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. eṭuttukkāṭṭākap pattaṭukkukaḷ, nūṟaṭukkukaḷ, āyira aṭukkukaḷ yāvum (u)=2o10=20, (uḷa)=2o100=200,         (ucū )=2o1000=2000 eṉṟavāṟu) perukkak kuṟippukaḷāvatu kāṇalām.
iṉṟiya ulakiṉ patiṉmāṉa varivaṭiva muṟikku itu mikavum aṇukkamāṉatu. āyiṉum, itaṟkum appatiṉmāṉa muṟikkum iṭiyē oru mika mukkiyamāṉa vēṟupāṭu uṇṭu. atuvē tāṉeṇkaḷ taṉik kuṟiyīṭukaḷāy amiyāmal, cuṉṉaṅkaḷiṉ eṇṇikkiyāl iṭakkuṟiyīṭu āka amivatu ākum.
tamiḻiṉ māṟṟu ilakka muṟi
tamiḻ ilakkattilēyē, mēlē aṭṭavaṇiyil aṭippuk kuṟikaḷukkuḷ kāṭṭappaṭṭa ilakkamuṟi ivvakiyil amintatē. atu tamiḻakattil peruvaḻakkāka muṉpu vaḻaṅkāta muṟiyāyiṉum, ciṟupāṉmi vaḻakkākat tamiḻakattilēyē tōṟṟiya oru māṟṟu muṟiyē eṉṉalām.
kīḻvarum ceytikaḷ itic cuṭṭikkāṭṭa utavukiṉṟaṉa.
1. 	ilakkaṅkaḷiṉ eḻuttumuṟik kuṟipput tāṉa ilakkaṅkaḷiyē vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka munnūṟṟu irupattu ēḻu eṉṟa eṇṇil 3,2,7 ākiya ilakkaṅkaḷiyum munnūṟṟu ēḻu eṉpatil 3,0,7 eṉac cuṉṉam uṭpaṭṭa ilakkaṅkaḷiyumē atu vaḻaṅkukiṟatu.
2. 	vāṉūṟ kaṇippil paral eṇṇumiṭattil paraṟkōṭukaḷ tāṉaṅ kaḷākavum ilakkaṅkaḷ tāṉa ilakkaṅkaḷākavumē kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. uṇmiyil patiṉmāṉa muṟiyiṉ kaṭicip paṭiyāṉa cuṉṉa marapu tōṟṟiya vakiyē iḵtu eṉṟu kūṟalām.
3. 	piṟa teṉṉāṭṭut tamiḻiṉa moḻikaḷāṉa maliyāḷam, teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam ākiyavi cuṉṉa marapuṭaṉ kūṭiya māṟṟumuṟiyē vaḻaṅku kiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟic camaṟkiruta tēvanākariyiliruntō ulaka marapiliruntō avi peṟavilli. ēṉeṉil, ivi am moḻikaḷukkē yuriya taṉi ilakka muṟikaḷ.
4. 	teṉṉāṭṭil aṇimiyilēyē tēvanākarika eḻuttu muṟi paravat toṭaṅkiyuḷḷatu. aṇimivari camaṟ kirutattiṉ eḻuttu muṟiyāka iruntu vantuḷḷa eḻuttu muṟi kiranta eḻuttu muṟiyē. itaṉ ilakka muṟiyil oṉṟu mutal oṉpatu variyuḷḷa ilakkak kuṟiyīṭukaḷ tamiḻ ilakkak kuṟiyīṭukaḷē. āyiṉum, tamiḻiṉ māṟṟu muṟiyākiya cuṉṉamuṟiyē tāṉaṅkaḷukkup payaṉ paṭuttap paṭukiṟatu.
5. 	tamiḻ ilakkamuṟi muḻu eṇkaḷākiya mēlvāy eṇkaḷukku maṭṭumiṉṟi, kīḻvāy eṇkaḷukkum virivāṉa muṟiyil amin tuḷḷatu. ari, kāl eṉṟa muṟiyil iraṇṭiraṇṭāṉa pākupāṭṭu muṟi kālvīcam allatu aṟupattu nālil oṉṟuvari celkiṟatu. itil aintil oru paṅkāṉa muntiri munnūṟṟirupatil oṉṟākum. itil munnūṟṟirupatil oṉṟāṉa kīḻ muntiriyum, .itil irupattoṉṟil iraṇṭu paṅkāṉa immiyum tamiḻiṉ ciṟṟellik kīḻvāy ākum. ivaṟṟil muntirivari ilakkak kuṟiyīṭu uṇṭu.
kīḻvāyilum patiṉmāṉattukku aṭippaṭiyāṉa kuṟiyīṭu tamiḻil uṇṭu. mūṉṟil oṉṟum ēḻil oṉṟumkūṭa immivari ceṉṟu aḷaviṭak kūṭiyaviyē.
pāti pātiyāṉa tamiḻk kīḻvāyp pākupāṭu kiṭṭattaṭṭa ulakeṅkum parantuḷḷatu. āṉāl, kāl allatu nālil oṉṟuṭaṉ perumpālāṉa intiya, ulaka moḻikaḷum, vīcam, kālvīcam, kāl vīcattiṉ mūṉṟil oṉṟu (atāvatu nūṟṟut toṇṇūṟṟiraṇṭil oṉṟāṉa pi) vari kucarāttiyum tamiḻk kīḻvāy marapip piṉpaṟṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
kīḻvāyil ellā moḻikaḷiyum toṉṟu toṭṭiruntē kaṭanta tamiḻ moḻiyē mēlvāyilum muṉṉōṭic ceṉṟu, ulaka moḻikaḷil appaṇpip parappiṉa eṉṉalām.
ulaka eṇmāṉam
mēlē kāṭṭiya parunilic ceytikaḷ, ulaka eṇmāṉattiṉ tōṟṟam maṭṭumiṉṟi, vaḷarcciyum muṟṟilum tamiḻakam cārntateṉpati nilināṭṭap pōtiyaṉa allavāyiṉum, atic cuṭṭikkāṭṭa pōtiyavi. varuṅkāla ārāycci ataṉit teḷivupaṭutti mēlum viḷakkam taralām.
mēliyulakam eṇmāṉattic camaṟkiruta tēva nākari mūlamum putiya arapu eṇmūlamumē peṟṟatu eṉpatu teḷivu.
patiṉmāṉattiṉ varivaṭivilum eḻuttiṉ varivaṭivilum intiyāvukku veḷiyē āriya iṉattiṉ paṅku etuvum illi. attuṭaṉ, intiyāvilum aḵtu āriya moḻiyiṉattiṉāl tōṟṟuvikkappaṭṭataṉṟu; vaḷarkkappaṭṭatu kūṭa aṉṟu eṉpati itu kuṟittuk kāṭṭa vallatu. teṉṉāṭṭu varalāṟu, paḻamporuḷ ārāycci, moḻiyārāycci pōtiya vaḷarcci yaṭiyāmalum, vaḷarcciyaṭinta aḷavilum veḷiyulakat toṭarpō, viḷakkamō kāṇāmalum iruppataṉāl, itaṉi muḻutum nilināṭṭap pōtiya ātāraṅkaḷ kiṭṭa vaḻiyillātirukkiṟatu.
āṉāl, patiṉmāṉak karuttup paṭivattilum olip paṭivattilum iṉṟiya nākarika ulakiṉ oṟṟumi viyattaṟkuriyatu. ivvaṭiṅkaḷ vari vaṭivattuṭaṉ muṟṟilum ottu viḷaṅkuvatu iraṇṭu moḻikaḷil maṭṭumē. oṉṟu ikkāla mēli moḻikaḷil oṉṟākiya āṅkilam, maṟṟoṉṟu paṇṭik kīḻi moḻikaḷuḷ oṉṟāṉa tamiḻ, maṟṟa enta moḻiyilum ivi ottu iyalavilli.
eṭuttukkāṭṭāka irupattu nālu eṉpatil tamiḻc collilum āṅkilac collilum irupatu mutalilum nāṉku piṉṉum varukiṉṟaṉa. eḻuttu vaṭivilum ippaṭiyē eḻutukiṟōm. iraṇṭu mutalilum nālu piṉṉumē ulaka eṇmāṉattil uḷḷaṉa. āṉāl, airōppāviṉ maṟṟa ikkāla moḻikaḷilum ilattīṉ, kirēkka moḻikaḷilum camaṟkirutattilum tamiḻiṉam nīṅkalāṉa intiya moḻikaḷilum olivaṭivil nāṉku mutalilum irupatu piṉṉumē varukiṉṟaṉa. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka camaṟkirutattil irupattunāṉku caturvimcati ākum. catur eṉpatu nāṉku; vimcati eṉpatu irupatu.
arapu mutaliya mēli āciya moḻikaḷil eḻutuvatu iṭamiruntu valamalla; valamiruntu iṭam. eṉavē, ammoḻikaḷil eṇmāṉa oli marapum varivaṭivu marapum oṉṟāy amikiṟatu. patiṉmāṉa ilakkamuṟi arapu mūlam uṭiyatu eṉpataṟku itu muṉpu ātāramākak koḷḷappaṭṭatu. āṉāl, ūṉṟik kavaṉittāl itu muṟṟilum cariyaṉṟu. immoḻikaḷ uṭpaṭa ellā moḻikaḷilum pattaṭukkilum nūṟaṭukkilum (irupatu, irunūṟu pōlvaṉavaṟṟil) ciṟṟeṇ mutalilum pēreṇ piṉṉumē varukiṉṟaṉa. valamiruntu iṭam cellum muṟiyil iṅkē olivaṭivattukku varivaṭivam māṟāki viṭukiṟatu.
tamiḻum cuṉṉa muṟiyum 
tamiḻar cuṉṉa muṟiyik kaṇṭu māṟṟu ilakka muṟiyi vakuttālum, atip perukkamāka vaḻaṅkātataṟku oru kāraṇam uṇṭu. māṟṟu muṟiyiviṭap paḻiya muṟi karuttu vaṭivattuṭaṉum avvaṭivattuṭaṉum mikavum iyivatu. varivaṭivak kaṇakku muṟiyil muṉṉatu vāyppā yiruntālum, maṉakkaṇakku muṟiyil piṉṉatē mikutiyum vāyppuṭiyatu. tavira kīḻvāy ilakka muṟi muḻutum patiṉmāṉa muṟi āyviṭāta niliyil, paḻiya ilakka muṟi ataṉuṭaṉ eḷitil iyipuṭiyatāy amintatu.
maṉakkaṇakku muṟiyil tamiḻar aṇimivari  tiṟamimikkavareṉap peyar peṟṟiruntaṉar. vāṇikattil atu mikavum payaṉpaṭṭatu.
mēlvāy eṇ patiṉmāṉa aṭippaṭiyākkappaṭṭu ulakeṅkum toṭakkappaḷḷikaḷil eṇcuvaṭi uruvil iṉṟu maṉappāṭam ceyyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻakattil mēṉāṭṭuk kalvimuṟi vantu ippaṇpu keṭukkum vari, kīḻvāyilum itēvaki nuṇmāṉa muṟip perukkal, vakuttal, kūṭṭal, kaḻittal. patiṉmāṉattāṉa ēṟṟam, taṟperukkam allatu kuḻipperukkam ākiyavi payilappaṭṭuvantaṉa.
kīḻvāy ilakkattil iṉṟukūṭa, maṟṟa muṟikaḷuṭaṉ tamiḻ muṟi payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭāl ataṟkut taṉicciṟappuṇṭu. kuṟippākak kīḻvāyp potu eṇṇip patiṉmāṉak kīḻvāyākkuvatil tamiḻk kīḻvāy eṇ-muṟiyum ataṉ eṇcuvaṭiyum patiṉmāṉa ēṟṟamum perumpayaṉuṭiyaṉavākum. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka eṭṭil mūṉṟil (3/8) patiṉmāṉam kāṇakkāṇa ataṉ tamiḻ nuṇmāṉattip (kālēyarikkāl) patiṉtāṉaṅkaḷāl muḻu eṇ ākum vari, (kālē arikkāl, mūṉṟēmukkāl, muppattēḻari, munnūṟṟeḻupantintu eṉṟu) ēṟṟiṉāl patiṉmāṉam (375) eḷitil kiṭikkum.
eṇṇum eḻuttum
‘eṇṇum eḻuttum iru kaṇṇeṉat takum’ eṉpatu avvi tiruvākku.``eṇṇeṉpa, ēṉi eḻutteṉpa; ivviraṇṭum-kaṇṇeṉpa vāḻum uyirkku” eṉṟu vaḷḷuvar iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉpē vakuttuc ceṉṟuḷḷār.
iv vāymoḻiyil evvaḷavu pēruṇmi potintuḷḷatu eṉpati ulaka moḻiyārāycciyāḷar maṭṭumē teḷḷat teḷiyak kāṇakkūṭum. tamiḻulakam ulakukkut tanta celvaṅkaḷ palappala. āṉāl, avaṟṟil taliciṟanta celvaṅkaḷ eṇṇum eḻuttumē. avaṟṟit tamiḻiṉampōlap pēṇiya iṉamum piṟitilli eṉṉalām.
iṉṟu ulakil kīḻnāṭukaḷum tamiḻakamum kalviyil maṭṭumaṉṟi aṭip paṭik kalviyāṉa eṇṇeḻuttaṟivilum mikavum piṟpaṭṭēyuḷḷaṉa. āṉāl, itu nēraṭiyākat tamiḻiṉattiṉ kuṟiyaṉṟu. tamiḻar kuṭiyāṭcik kālattil vaḷarttu ulakeṅkum parappiya cīriya paṇpukaḷ, muṭiyāṭci ēṟpaṭṭa kālamutal paṭippaṭiyākac cīraḻivuṟṟu, ayaliṉa ayal paṇpāṭukaḷukkum, paṇpāṭṭil kuṟintōriṉ ātikkattukkum vaḻivakuttuḷḷatu. iṉṟiya nili nīṭitta aṭimip paṇpiṉ viḷivēyākum.
paḻiya kāla niliyic caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ namakkup paṭam piṭittuk kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. ikkālattavar ati uḷḷavāṟu kāṇātatum kāṇa eṇṇātatum aṭimi maṉappāṉmi yiṉālēyē eṉṉalām. accup poṟi ēṟpaṭāta annāḷil paṉiyōliyaṉṟi eḷiya eḻutuporuḷ kāṇappaṭāta niliyil, kalvikkuriya vacatikaḷ ulakeṅkum kuṟivākavē iruntirukka muṭiyum eṉpatu teḷivu. āṉāl, ittakiya kālattilum tamiḻar pērilakkiya ilakkaṇa vaḷam ey tiyiruntaṉar. ataṉ oru ciṟu pakutiyē tolkāppiya vaṭivilum, caṅka ilakkiya vaṭivilum nammi vanteṭṭiyuḷḷaṉa. eṭṭiyuḷḷa pakutiyē iṉṟaḷavum ulakil īṭiṇiyaṟṟa viyattaku paṇpukaḷi uṭiyaṉa.
caṅka ilakkiyam kuṟippiṭattakka niliyilum aḷavilum ulakiṉ mikac ciṟanta camayañ cārā uyar ilakkiyamākak kāṭciyaḷikkiṟatu. atēcamayam ōr uyarnta nākarikattiyum aṟivu oḻukkap paṇpukaḷiyum viḷakkum oppaṟṟa kālak kaṇṇāṭiyākavum, makkaḷ ilakkiyamākavum, tēciya ilakkiyamākavum atu viḷaṅkukiṟatu.
kalvi ciṟanta tamiḻakam
iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻakattil kalvi enta aḷavu nāṭeṅkum, camutāyappaṭi muḻuvatum āṇ, peṇ vēṟupāṭiṉṟip paraviyiruntatu eṉpatic caṅka ilakkiyam kāṭṭukiṟatu. kiṭṭiya ilakkiyattiṉ kiṭṭiya pakuti iraṇṭu mūṉṟu talimuṟikku uṭpaṭṭatē. āyiṉum, tamiḻakattiṉ iṉṟiya māvaṭṭam oṉṟu tavaṟāmal iṉṟiya tamiḻakattukku veḷiyēyuḷḷa pakutikaḷkūṭa atil pulavariṉ tāyakamāy iruntuḷḷaṉa. pulavarkaḷ, pāṇar vakuppilum aracariḷavacariṭi yilum vaṇikariṭiyilum maruttuvariṭiyilum vēṟṟumi yiṉṟip paraviyiruntatu. caṅkap pulavar ainnūṟṟukkuc ciṟitu mēṟpaṭṭavariṭiyē muppatukku mēṟpaṭṭavar atāvatu nūṟṟukku āṟu viḻukkāṭu peṇṭirākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷum eyiṉar, pēyar, kuṟavar, araciḷañcelviyar ākiya ellāc camutāya vakuppup paṭikaḷukkum ellā māvaṭṭaṅka ḷukkum uriyarākavē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. mēli ulakil iṉṟuḷḷa araciyal, aṟiviyal moḻittuṟi vāyppu vaḷaṅkaḷiṭiyēkūṭa, itaṟkoppāṉa niliyi enta nāṭāvatu kāṭṭa muṭiyumā eṉpatu āyntu kāṇattakka ceytiyē yākum.
ippāriya kalvi muyaṟcikku aṭippaṭiyāṉatu caṅka vāḻvu. cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyar vakutta kalvik kōṭṭiyiṉ koṭumuṭiyē, koṭikkampamē atu. atuvē kalvit tuṟiyil oru tēciya, iṉap palkalikkaḻakamākavum, aṟivut tuṟiyil ōr ārāyccik kūṭamākavum, ilakkiyat tuṟiyil ulakaḷāviya aruḷaṟac cāliyākavum iruntu nāṭu kaṭanta tamiḻar tēciya vāḻviyum ulakap paṇpiyum vaḷarttatu.
cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyar āṭcikku veḷiyēyum, avvāṭcik kālaṅ kaṭantu, tēciya vāḻviṉ taḷarccik kālaṅkaḷilum, ikkalvi ēṟpāṭukaḷ paṭippaṭiyākat taḷarvuṟṟaṉa. āṉāl, iṉṟum tamiḻaracarkaḷuḷ cērar kuṭi irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭuvari nīṭittuḷḷa tiruvāṅkūr kocciyil kalvi mika muṉippākavum, pāṇṭiyar cōḻar nīṭittāṇṭa tamiḻakap pakutiyil aṭutta paṭi muṉippākavumē uḷḷaṉa eṉpatu kūrntu kavaṉikkattakkatu.
tamiḻar taṭṭukkeṭṭa kālattil kalvimuṟiyi vakuttup pēṇa nallaracukaḷ illi. āṉāl, accamayattilum kalviyil makkaḷum makkaṭ kaviñarum perumuyaṟci mēṟkoṇṭiruntaṉar. piṟkāla avviyāratu ātticūṭi, koṉṟivēntaṉ, ulakanīti, mūturi, nalvaḻi ākiyaviyum, ativīrarāma pāṇṭiyaratu veṟṟi vēṟkiyum, civappirakācaratu naṉṉeṟiyum, kumarakuruparatu nītineṟi viḷakkamum immuyaṟcikkuc cāṉṟukaḷ ākum. paḷḷi vakuppukaḷi etirnōkkip paṭippaṭiyākat tiṭṭamiṭappaṭṭa ittakiya ēṭukaḷ, iṉṟum ilakkiyat tuṟiyil emmoḻiyilum eḷitil kāṇamuṭiyātavi ākum.
makkaṭ kaviñariṉ ikkalvi maṟumalarcci āṅkila āṭci toṭaṅku vataṟku muṉ, 16-17ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkuriyatākum. maṟumalarccikku āṅkila āṭci utaviṟṟāyiṉum, ataṉ vērmutal atuvaṉṟu eṉpati itu kāṭṭukiṟatu.


5. tamiḻ ilakkaṇamum moḻi nūlum
kāṟṟu maṇṭalattil paṟakkum paṟavi kāṟṟippaṟṟi eṇṇātu. nīrāḻattil iyaṅkum mīṉiṉamum nīriyaṟiyātu. atupōlavē maṉitaṉ moḻiyil piṟantu, moḻiyil iyaṅki vaḷarntālum, moḻiyippaṟṟip perumpālum eṇṇuva tilli. taṉakkeṉa oru moḻi uṇṭu eṉpatikkūṭa, iṉṉoru moḻiyik kāṇumpōtutāṉ avaṉ uṇara muṭiyum. pala moḻikaḷukku ayal moḻiyāḷar toṭarpu ēṟpaṭṭa piṉpē peyar amivataṉ kāraṇam ituvē.
moḻiyip paṟṟi eṇṇāmalē maṉitaṉ talimuṟi talimuṟiyāka moḻiyi ākkukiṟāṉ. ākki vaḷarkkiṟāṉ. ivi avaṉ aṟiyāc ceyalkaḷ. uṇṭa uṇavic cerimāṉam ceyvatu vaḷarvatu ākiya uyirc ceyalkaḷ pōṉṟavi ivi. moḻiyil iyaṅkum ivvaṟiyāc ceyalkaḷi yaṭuttu, moḻiyi yiyakkum aṟiyāc ceyalilum, moḻiyi uṇarntārāyum aṟivuc  ceyalilum avaṉ īṭupaṭukiṟāṉ. moḻiyi iyakkum avaṉ aṟiyā uṇarvuc ceyalē ilakkiyam. moḻiyi uṇarntārāyum avaṉ aṟivuc ceyalē ilakkaṇam, moḻi nūl.
ilakkiya ūḻi :
iṉa, camutāya, taṉi maṉita ilakkiyap paṭikaḷ
maṉitaṉ eḻuttik kāṇumuṉ, kuṭivāḻvu peṟṟuc camutāyamāka amiyumuṉ, nāṭōṭi vāḻkkiyilēyē eḻutā iṉa ilakkiyam ākkiṉāṉ. itu peritum avaṉ viḷimuṟic camaya vāḻvu cārntatāyiruntatu. pāracīka āriyariṉ maṟi nūlākiya ave°tāvum, intiya āriyariṉ maṟinūlākiya irukku vētamum maṉita iṉa varalāṟṟil kālattāl pintiya iṭikkālat taviyāṉālum, taṉmiyāl im mutaṟpaṭi cārnta iṉa ilakkiyaṅkaḷēyākum.
kuṭivāḻvum camutāyamum amintapiṉ iṉa ilakkiyam camutāya ilakkiyamākiṟatu. iḵtu eḻutāc camutāya ilakkiyamē. ēṉeṉil, ituvum eḻutappaṭāmal marapu vaḻiyē vaḷarntu vantatākum. nāṭṭup pāṭalkaḷ. vīrak katikaḷ, camūkap paḻaṅ katikaḷ, camaya viḷimuṟi ilakkiyaṅkaḷ, āṭalpāṭal, nāṭaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ ākiyavi intap paṭiyic cārntavi.
tamiḻaṉṉiyākiya avviyār pōṟṟiya ēṟṟappāṭal ivvakippaṭṭa camutāyap pāṭalēyākum. kuṟavañci, paḷḷu, ammāṉi mutaliya tamiḻt tuṟikaḷum immarapiṉavē.
ivviraṇṭu paṭikaḷilum moḻiyiṉ uyirik kāṇalām. iṉam allatu camutāyattiṉ vaḷarcciyik kāṇalām. āṉāl, ivaṟṟil taṉi maṉitaṉ iṭam peṟuvatilli. iṉat talimuṟikaḷum camutāyat talimuṟikaḷumē avaṟṟi ākkiyiyakki vaḷarkkiṉṟaṉa. camaṟkiruta purāṇa, itikāca, cumirutikaḷ camutāya ilakkiyaṅkaḷil piṟkālat tokuppēyākum.
iṉa āṭciyilum camutāya āṭciyilum vīrar, maṉṉar, kurumār iṭam peṟṟaṉar. āṉāl, ivarkaḷ camutāyattil oru marapiṉarāka āṇṭaṉarēyaṉṟi, iṉa vāḻvi iyakkavilli. taṉit taṉmiyum peṟavilli. ivarkaḷuḷ evar peyarkaḷum nammi vantu eṭṭātataṉ kāraṇam ituvē. āṭci ceytavar tam āṭcik kālattuṭaṉ maṟintaṉar. putiya āṭciyāḷar pukaḻ putiya āṭcik kālattil nilaviṟṟu.
mutal mutal iṉa vāḻviyō camutāya vāḻviyō talimuṟi kaṭantu talimuṟiyāka iyakkat talippaṭṭa taṉi maṉitarkaḷ palar. ivarkaḷē iṉa mutalvar, kulamutalvar eṉṟum, teyvaṅkaḷ eṉṟum, camaya mutalvar allatu ilakkiya mutalvar eṉṟum pala peyarkaḷāl ciṟappikkappaṭṭaṉar. pāracīka nāṭṭil jaratuṣṭirar, cīṉattil kaṉpiyūciyacu, yūtariṭiyē, mōcē āpirakām ākiyōr ittakiya iṉap periyār āvar. ivarkaḷiṭiyē kaṉpiyūciyacum jaratuṣṭirarum ilakkiya mutalvarākavum viḷaṅkiṉar.
kirēkka nāṭṭāratu vāḻvu hōmar eṉṟa camutāyak kaviñari ilakkiya mutalvarākak koṇṭē toṭaṅkiṟṟu.
eḻuttaminta piṉṉarē camutāyattil taṉi maṉitaṉ moḻiyi iyakki ilakkiyam vaḷarkkat toṭaṅkiṉāṉ. accuppoṟi  ēṟpaṭṭapiṉ taṉi maṉitaṉ ilakkiyam nāṭṭilakkiyam, ulaka ilakkiyamāka vaḷarntu varukiṟatu.
maṉita ulakilē, tamiḻ mikap paḻimi vāynta moḻi eṉpatilē aiyamilli. āṉāl, maṉita iṉap paḻimiyiṉ paṭikaḷit tamiḻil teḷivākak kāṇamuṭiyātu. irukku vētap paṭi, jaratuṣṭirar kaṉpiyūciyacu paṭikaḷiyō āpirakām hōmar paṭikaḷiyō varalāṟṟukkum ārāyccikkum eṭṭāta tol paḻaṅ kālattilēyē tamiḻ kaṭantuviṭṭatu. irukku vētak kālattukku muṉṉarē tamiḻar eḻuttu muṟikaḷilum, taṉimaṉitaṉ ilakkiya ilakkaṇa vaḷarcciyilum neṭuntoli muṉṉēṟiviṭṭaṉar.
maṉita iṉattiṉ muṟpaṭṭa ilakkiya, moḻippaṭikaḷi nām nākarikattil pintiviṭṭa piṟa nāṭukaḷilum iṉaṅkaḷilumē tēṭa vēṇṭiyiruppataṉ kāraṇam ituvē. tamiḻil ivaṟṟiṉ taṭaṅkaḷ uṇṭu. viḷakkaṅkaḷip piṟa moḻikaḷilēyē nāṭutal vēṇṭum.
ilakkiyattukkuriya paṭi maṉitar iṉa uṇarvic cuṭṭukiṟa oru paṭiyēyākum. ilakkaṇattiṉ paṭiyō ataṟkappāṟ paṭṭatu. atuvē maṉitaṉ moḻiyuṇarvu peṟṟa paruvam ākum. ilakkaṇamiṉṟi ilakkiyam maṭṭumē vaḷarcciyaṭintirunta kālatti nām ilakkiya ūḻi eṉṟum, ilakkaṇam tōṉṟiya piṉṉuḷḷa kālatti ilakkaṇa ūḻi eṉṟum kūṟalām.
ilakkaṇa ūḻi: mēli moḻikaḷ
tolkāppiyak kālattukku neṭunāḷ muṟpaṭṭē inta ilakkiya ūḻiyiyum ilakkaṇa ūḻiyiyum tamiḻ kaṭantuviṭṭatu.
tamiḻarōṭotta paḻimiyuṭiya tolpaḻaṅkāla iṉattavarāṉa ekiptiyarum cumēriyarum cālṭiyarum piṟa naṭunilak kaṭal iṉattavarum ilakkaṇa ūḻiyi eṭṭavē yilli. ilakkiya ūḻiyilkūṭat taṉimaṉita ūḻiyi avarkaḷ aṭiyavilli.  iṉa ilakkiya, camutāya ilakkiyap paṭiyilēyē avarkaḷ vāḻntu māḷvuṟṟu maṭintaṉar.
kirēkkarum urōmarum ilakkiya ūḻiyil neṭun toli ceṉṟu muṉṉēṟiṉar. ilakkaṇa ūḻiyi iṟuti yilēyē ceṉṟu eṭṭiṉar. ilakkaṇa ilakkiyamillāta paṇpaṭā moḻikaḷuṭaṉ avarkaḷukku ēṟpaṭṭa toṭarpē vērkaḷiṭiyē ilakkaṇa uṇarvi vaḷarttatu. ciṟappāka immoḻikaḷil vāḻvu aḻinta piṉ, mēlināṭṭup paṇpaṭāt tāymoḻiyāḷar avaṟṟi ayalmoḻikaḷākak kaṟpikkavēṇṭi vantatu. perumpālum oru moḻiyāḷar kaḷiṉ kaṇṇōṭṭattuṭaṉ kāṇumpōtutāṉ ataṉ ilakkaṇa vaṭivam iṉṟiyamiyāc ciṟappuṭiyatāyamiyum. ilattīṉ moḻi taṉippaṭṭum, kirēkkamoḻi aṭuttapaṭiyākavum ivvakiyil tam paṇpaṭā ayalmoḻi māṇavarālēyē peritum ilakkaṇa vaki tokic ciṟappu aṭintatu.
iṉṟiya mēlināṭṭu moḻikaḷ toṭakkattil ilattīṉ kirēkka moḻikaḷukku aminta ilakkaṇap paṭivaṅkaḷiyē tam vakiyilum piṉpaṟṟiṉa. it tavaṟuṇarntu tāymoḻikaḷ taṉittaṉi ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ vakuttapōtum, avaṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭi amitikaḷ ilattīṉ kirēkkamoḻi amitikaḷākavē amintaṉa. vēṇṭiya vēṇṭiya iṉṟiyamiyā māṟutalkaḷuṭaṉ avi avvat tāymoḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇaṅkaḷāy amintaṉa; amintuvarukiṉṟaṉa.
mēṉāṭṭu moḻikaḷil ilakkaṇaṅkaḷiṉ uyirp paṇpum toṭakkap paṇpum iṅṅaṉam ilattīṉ kirēkka ilakkaṇap paṇpukaḷākavē amintuḷḷaṉa.


epirēya, arapumoḻi ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ
naṭunilak kaṭalulakiṉ iṭikkāla moḻikaḷuḷ epirēya moḻiyum arapumoḻiyum maṭṭumē ilakkaṇa ilakkiya vaḷarcci peṟṟaṉa. kiṟittuva maṟikkum iculāmiya maṟikkum avi tāyaka moḻikaḷāy amintiruntaṉa. ākavētāṉ, avi ivvaḷarcciyil ūkkam mikuti peṟṟaṉa. āṉāl, ivvilakkaṇattil perumpakuti eḻuttilakkaṇamākavum, aṭutta ciṟupakuti collākka ilakkaṇamākavum maṭṭumē amintatu. moḻiyiṉ oli māṟupaṭuntōṟum varivaṭivu aṭippaṭiyil māṟupaṭāmal tiripuṟṟatāl, kālañ cellac cella, eḻuttaṟivukku immāṟupāṭṭaṟivu tēvippaṭṭatu. inniliyi āṅkila moḻi ōraḷavu cuṭṭik kāṭṭum.
iṉṟiya āṅkila eḻuttumuṟi 14ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ olippu muṟippaṭi amintatu. ataṉpiṉ accu ēṟpaṭṭatu. olikaḷ māṟiṉa. āṉāl, eḻuttu muṟi māṟavilli. katti eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum āṅkilac colliṉ paḻiya olippu knīḵvē (mankina) eṉpatu, eḻuttu vaṭivil iṉṟum aḵtu ippaṭiyē eḻutappaṭukiṟatu. āṉāl, iṉṟiya olippu muṟippaṭi atu niḵv eṉṟa col ākiṟatu. ip putiya collil ‘k’eṉṟa mey oli maṟintu ūmi eḻuttāyviṭukiṟatu. ‘ī’ eṉṟa uyiroli ‘ai’ eṉa māṟupaṭṭuviṭṭatu. iṟuti ēkāram olippiḻantu ūmi eḻuttāyiṟṟu. ittakiya oli māṟupāṭu kaḷāl coṟkaḷum moḻiyum kiṭṭattaṭṭa putuccoṟkaḷ, putumoḻi āyiṉa.
im māṟupāṭukaḷi āṅkilēyar tam eḻuttumuṟiyil kāṭṭa villi. eṉavētāṉ, iṉṟum ayalmoḻiyāḷar maṭṭumaṉṟi āṅkila nāṭṭup paḷḷic ciṟuvar, ciṟumiyarum ovvoru colliṉ eḻuttiyum olippiyum neṭṭuruc ceyyavēṇṭiyuḷḷatu.
epirēyarum arāpiyarum āṅkilēyarippōl eḻuttu muṟiyil piṟpaṭṭuviṭavilli. āṉāl, paḻiya eḻuttu muṟiyik kiviṭṭu viṭavumilli. paḻiya eḻutti appaṭiyē vittukkoṇṭu māṟupāṭṭik kuṟiyīṭukaḷāl kāṭṭiṉar. ituvē avarkaḷ pāriya eḻuttilakkaṇamāyiṟṟu. paḷḷic ciṟuvarum moḻi kaṟpōrum colliṉ eḻuttukaḷi neṭṭuruc ceyyavēṇṭuvatilli; āṉāl, eḻuttilakkaṇam kaṟṟātal vēṇṭum.
immoḻikaḷiṉ collākka ilakkaṇamum peritum ayal moḻi yāḷar moḻi kaṟpataṟku payaṉpaṭuvatāyiṟṟu. itaṉāl, aḵtu iṉṟi yamiyā iyaṟki vaḷarcci peṟṟatu.
camaṟkiruta ilakkaṇamum pāṇiṉiyum
intiyāviluḷḷa moḻikaḷuḷ paḻimiyāṉa ilakkiya ilakkaṇam vāynta moḻikaḷ iraṇṭē iraṇṭutām. oṉṟu camaṟkirutam, maṟṟoṉṟu tamiḻ, ivaṟṟuḷ camaṟkiruta ilakkaṇattiṉ ciṟappum pukaḻum varalāṟum ōraḷavu ulakaṟintavi. tamiḻilakkaṇattiṉ ciṟappum pukaḻum varalāṟumō iṉṉum pōtiya aḷavu tamiḻakamē aṟiyātavi. ulakam avaṟṟi aṟiyum nāḷilēyē tamiḻakamum avaṟṟiṉ ciṟappi aṟiyakkūṭum eṉṉalām.
nalla kālamāka, irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭil ulakam ammuṟiyil viḻippuṟat toṭaṅkiyuḷḷatu.
ulaka ilakkaṇaṅkaḷuḷ camaṟkiruta ilakkaṇattiṉ ciṟappum matippum peritu. itaṟku mūla kāraṇamāṉa camaṟkiruta ilakkaṇam pāṇiṉi muṉivar iyaṟṟiyatākak kūṟappaṭum pāṇiṉiyam allatu aṣṭātyāyiyē yākum.
itu curuṅkac colli viḷaṅkavippatil ulakiṉ oppuyarvaṟṟa nūl. itaṟkeṉap pāṇiṉi vakuttuk koṇṭa kuṟiyīṭṭumuṟi iṉṟum kaṇakkiyal mutaliya uyar aṟivu nūlkaḷukku iṉṟiyamiyāta oru karuvi muṟi ākum. itaṉit tiṟampaṭak kiyāṇṭavarkaḷuḷ kālattāl mutalvarum, taṉmiyāl mutaṉmiyāṉavarum pāṇiṉiyē eṉpatil aiyamilli. oru cila nūṟu cūttiraṅkaḷ allatu nūṟpāk kaḷukkuḷ avar vēṟu emmoḻikkum iṉṟuvari illāta oru muḻuniṟi ilakkaṇattic camaṟkiruta moḻikku ākki aḷittuḷḷār.
pāṇiṉiyatti otta ōr ilakkaṇam iṉṟum emmoḻikkum eḻutappaṭṭu viṭavilli eṉṟu kūṟuvatu mikiyākātu. ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil camaṟkiruta ilakkaṇap pulavarākavum, āṅkila muṟi moḻi nūlaṟiña rākavum viḷaṅkiya irācarācavarmā maliyāḷa moḻikku ilakkaṇamāka iyaṟṟiya kēraḷa pāṇiṉiyam uṇmiyil pāṇiṉiyatti viṭa uyar vāṉatē. āṉāl, ataṉ ciṟappukkup pāṇiṉiyiṉ muṉmātiriyum moḻi nūl vaḷarcciyumē peritum kāraṇamāyamintaṉa. aḵtu irumarapukaḷiṉ oru ciṟanta iṇippēyaṉṟi, oru putup pāṇiṉiyiṉ taṉic ciṟappu marapaṉṟu.
pāṇiṉi vakutta ilakkaṇam ōr ilakkaṇam maṭṭumaṉṟu. aḵtu ulakukku oru putiya ilakkaṇak kōṭpāṭṭiyē vakuttuk koṭuttatu. antak kōṭpāṭṭiṉ kūṟukaḷik kīḻvarumāṟu vakukkalām.
1. 	ellāc coṟkaḷukkum vērmutal (tātu) uṇṭu.
2. 	vērmutalkaḷiliruntu ellāc coṟkaḷum pakuti, vikuti mutaliya uṟuppukaḷiṇintu tōṉṟal, tirital, keṭutal eṉṉum muṟippaṭi ākkappaṭupavi. 
3. 	oru moḻiyiṉ ilakkaṇam anta moḻiyiṉ ellāc coṟkaḷiṉ collākkattiyum, ataṟku utavum eḻuttu allatu olittiripukaḷiyum viḷakkutal vēṇṭum. 
viyakkattakka muṟiyilē, im mūṉṟu tattuvaṅkaḷiyum appaḻukkiṉṟi muḻuvatum piṉpaṟṟi, avar kālattil uḷḷa paḻiya camaṟkiruta moḻi allatu ‘pāṣi’kku muḻuniṟi ilakkaṇam vakutta aṟivu mēti pāṇiṉi. kūṭiyamaṭṭum ellāc coṟkaḷukkum vērmutalkaḷ kaṇṭu piṭittu avar tam ilakkaṇa nūlil tantuḷḷār. ellā vērc coṟkaḷiliruntum ellāc coṟkaḷiyum ākkik kāṭṭap pōtiya colluṟuppilakkaṇam, collilakkaṇam, oli allatu eḻuttut tiripilakkaṇam ākiyavaṟṟiyum tantuḷḷār. avar kālattil vaḻaṅkāta coṟkaḷiyum marapukaḷiyum uriyāciriyarkaḷ uric cūttiraṅkaḷālum uriyiṟkōṭal marapukaḷālum virittuḷḷar.
kirēkka ilattīṉ ilakkaṇap pulavariṉ aṟivut tiṟattiyē uccanili aṟivut tiṟamākak karutivantirunta mēli ulakattār, pāṇiṉi ilakkaṇattik kaṇṭu mūkkil ki vittaṉar. pāṇiṉikku ucca aṟivārāycci ulakam nīṇṭanāḷ tali vaṇaṅkiṟṟu!
pāṇiṉiyum moḻi nūlum
pāṇiṉikkuriya iṉṉoru ciṟappum, ataṟkut tāyk kāraṇamāṉa camaṟkiruta moḻiyiṉ oru ciṟappum mēli ulakattār viyappi iṉṉum perukkiṉa. iviyē pāṇiṉi tanta eḻuttiṉ piṟappilakkaṇamum, camaṟkiruta neṭuṅkaṇakkiṉ eḻuttumuṟi variciyum ākum.
camaṟkiruta eḻuttukaḷi uyirkaḷ, meykaḷ, uyir meykaḷ, kūṭṭu eḻuttukaḷ eṉa nāṉkākap pakuttu, uyirum meyyumē aṭippaṭi olikaḷ eṉṟu pāṇiṉi kāṭṭiṉār. atu maṭṭumō? avaṟṟiṉ piṟappiṭam, muyaṟci, tiripu ākiyavaṟṟiyum viḷakkiṉār.
itil pāṇiṉiyiṉ ciṟappu maṭṭumaṉṟi, camaṟkirutattiṉ ciṟappum peritākavē iruntatu. ēṉeṉil, pāṇiṉikku muṟpaṭṭē moḻiyiṉ eḻuttuvaṭivum, neṭuṅ kaṇakkiṉ eḻuttu varicimuṟiyum ivaṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭiyil amintiruntaṉa.
pāṇiṉi kāṭṭiya moḻi cārnta puttaṟivu mēli ulakil olinūl (ñāṭinēviuḷa), moḻinūl (ñāṭiṭiṭaṭipala) eṉṟa iru putunūl tuṟikaḷ tōṉṟak kāraṇamāy amintatu.
kirēkka moḻiyil uḷḷa mūṉṟu kakaraṅkaḷ (ma,o,la,)* ilattīṉ mutaliya piṟa mēli moḻikaḷiluḷḷa iraṇṭu kakaraṅkaḷ (ūallatu mu,ḻu) ākiya ivi orē oliyiṉ vaṉmiyum meṉmiyumē eṉpatip pāṇiṉi ilakkaṇamum camaṟkiruta moḻiyumē mēli ulaka ilakkaṇap pulavar kaḷukkum, moḻip pulavarkkum kāṭṭiṉa. kirēkka moḻi yiluḷḷa ekara ēkāraṅkaḷ (nu ṟa)ṅī okara ōkāraṅkaḷ (ṭī,ṟu)ṅa orē uyiriṉ kuṟil neṭil aḷavikaḷē eṉpatiyum mēlnāṭṭiṉar potuvākavum, āṅkilēyar ciṟappākavum itaṉ piṉṉarē uṇarntaṉar. kirēkkar itaṉi oru ciṟitē ūka muṟiyil uyttuṇarn tiruntaṉar. intiya ilakkaṇap pulavarpōla aḷaviyiṭṭu uṇaravilli.
19ām nūṟṟāṇṭil mēliyulakil putiya ilakkaṇa vaḷarccikkum puttam putiya nūlkaḷākiya oli nūl, moḻinūl ākiyavaṟṟiṉ vaḷarccikkum pāṇiṉi ilakkaṇam tali yūṟṟāyiṟṟu.
āriya iṉattiṉ mikap paḻimi vāynta moḻi, āriya camaya iṉap paṇpāṭukaḷik kāṭṭum moḻi eṉṟa muṟikaḷil camaṟkirutattukku ēṟkeṉavē ēṟpaṭṭirunta ciṟappuṭaṉ, pāṇiṉiyiṉ ilakkaṇac ciṟappum pāṇiṉikkup piṉ ēṟpaṭṭa camaṟkiruta ilakkiyac ciṟappum ulaka araṅkil am moḻiyiṉ matippi vāṉaḷāva uyarttiṉa.
pāṇiṉikku muṟpaṭṭa vaṭapula ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ
camaṟkirutavāṇar pāṇiṉiyē camaṟkirutattukku mutal mutal ilakkaṇam vakuttavar eṉṟu karutiṉar. tamiḻarum iṭikkālattil itaṟ kiṇiyāka akattiyarē tamiḻkku mutal ilakkaṇam vakuttār eṉṟu kūṟiṉar. iraṇṭu karuttukaḷum iṉṟu valiyuṟavu kuṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa. ēṉeṉṟāl, tamiḻil akattiyar ilakkaṇam eṉṟōr ilakkaṇam iṉṟilli. aḵtu iruntatu eṉpataṟkāṉa uṟutiyāṉa cāṉṟukaḷum kiṭiyā. atē camayam vaṭapulattil pāṇiṉikku muṟpaṭṭa ilakkaṇam iruntatuṇṭu. ivi vētaṅkaḷōṭu aṅkamāka iṇikkappaṭṭa pirāticākkiyaṅkaḷ ākum.
mēṉāṭṭu aṟiñar palar pirāticākkiyaṅkaḷuṭaṉ pāṇiṉi ilak kaṇatti oppiṭṭaṉar. pirāticākkiyaṅkaḷil pala pāṇiṉikku muṟpaṭṭavi eṉṟum, cilavē piṟpaṭṭavi eṉṟum kaṇṭaṉar. attuṭaṉ oppumiyāl pāṇiṉi paṟṟiya pala putiya ceytikaḷ veḷippaṭṭaṉa.
uyir eḻuttu, mey eḻuttu viṉiyiṉ palvēṟu kālap poruṇmit tiripukaḷ ākiyavaṟṟukkup pāṇiṉi peyariṭṭu vaḻaṅkavilli. kuṟiyīṭu kaḷiṭṭē vaḻaṅkiṉār. eṭuttuk kāṭṭāka uyireḻuttu ‘ac’ eṉappaṭum. iḵtu oru collaṉṟu. pāṇiṉi ilakkaṇattiṉ toṭakka cūttiraṅkaḷil ‘a’ mutal toṭaṅki ‘c’ eṉṟa aṭiyāḷak kuṟiyīṭu varumiṭam variyuḷḷa eḻuttukaḷ eṉpataṉ curukkak kuṟippē ‘ac’ eṉpatu. itupōlavē ‘hal’ eṉpatu mey. ‘ha’vil toṭaṅki ‘l’ eṉṟa aṭiyāḷak kuṟippu varuvatu vari uḷḷa eḻuttukaḷ eṉpataṉ curukkak kuṟippākavē ‘hal’ vaḻaṅkiṟṟu.
viṉivaṭivaṅkaḷukkum pāṇiṉiyiṉ ilakkaṇattil peyarkaḷ coṟkaḷāka amiyavilli. ‘liṭ,’ ‘luṭ’. ‘lēṭ,’ ‘liṅ,’ ‘luṅ’ eṉap pāṇiṉi olik kuṟiyīṭāka vakutta aṭiyāḷak kuṟippukaḷē peyarkaḷāy amintaṉa. ivi urukkaṇakkil payaṉpaṭuttappaṭum ‘ek°’ ‘oy’ kuṟiyīṭukaḷ pōṉṟa iṭukuṟikaḷēyaṉṟi vēṟalla. avi moḻiyiṉ coṟkaḷalla, pāṇiṉiyiṉ paṭippukaḷ.
pāṇiṉi virumpiya curukka viḷakkattukku ikkuṟiyīṭṭu muṟikaḷ mikavum utaviṉa. āṉāl, pirāticākkiyaṅkaḷil tamiḻilum maṟṟa moḻikaḷilum amintatu pōlap peyarkaḷ coṟkaḷākavē amintiruntaṉa.
tavira, pirāticākkiyaṅkaḷuṭaṉ pāṇiṉiyi oppiṭṭup pārttatil, eḻuttukaḷiṉ piṟappil pāṇiṉikkum pirāticākkiyaṅ kaḷukkum kuṟippiṭattakka vēṟṟumikaḷ iruntaṉa. pāṇiṉi olikaḷiṉ piṟappippaṟṟip pala tavaṟāṉa karuttukaḷik koṇṭiruntār eṉṟum, pirāticākkiyaṅkaḷ olikaḷiṉ piṟappi viḷakkuvatil mēmpaṭṭiruntaṉa eṉṟum kaṇṭaṉar.
oli nūliṉ tanti eṉṟu pāṇiṉi itaṟku muṉ karutap paṭṭiruntār. apperumi camaṟkiruta marapuk kuriyatēyaṉṟip pāṇiṉik kuriyataṉṟu eṉṟu avarkaḷ kaṇṭaṉar. pāṇiṉiyi viṭa mikutiyākap pirāticākkiyaṅkaḷi iyaṟṟiya āciriyar kaḷukkē atil perum paṅku uṇṭu eṉṟu 19-ām nūṟ ṟāṇṭiṟutiyiliruntu kāṇappaṭṭatu.
aintira ilakkaṇam
